Using the identified time-varying Koopman model, two controllers are developed: an adaptive Koopman-based model predictive controller (AKMPC) with a disturbance observer and a Koopman-based feedback linearization controller (K-FBLC), which serves as a benchmark. The controllers are evaluated for two control strategies, namely configurations of spool speeds and EPR, under both sea-level and varying flight conditions. The results demonstrate that the proposed identification approach enables accurate predictions of both spool speeds and EPR, allowing the Koopman model to be reused flexibly across different control formulations. While both control strategies achieve comparable performance in steady conditions, the AKMPC exhibits superior robustness compared with the K-FBLC under varying flight conditions due to its ability to compensate for model mismatch. Moreover, the EPR control strategy improves the thrust response. The study highlights the applicability of Koopman-based control and demonstrates the advantages of the AKMPC-based framework for robust turbofan engine control.

</details>


### [80] [FourierMoE: Fourier Mixture-of-Experts Adaptation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01762)
*Juyong Jiang,Fan Wang,Hong Qi,Sunghun Kim,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FourierMoE：一种在频域进行参数高效微调的新方法，通过傅里叶变换和MoE架构实现频率感知的专家适配，显著减少可训练参数的同时提升多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数高效微调方法在多任务设置中面临任务干扰和表示不足的问题，现有MoE方法主要在空间域操作，存在结构冗余和参数开销。研究发现不同任务具有不同的频率能量分布，LLM层对频率敏感度存在异质性，这为频域适配提供了理论基础。

Method: 提出FourierMoE，将MoE架构与逆离散傅里叶变换结合，实现频率感知适配。使用频率自适应路由器将token分配到专门处理不同频带的专家，每个专家学习一组共轭对称的复数系数，通过IDFT无损重构为实值空间权重。

Result: 在28个基准测试、多种模型架构和规模上的广泛评估表明，FourierMoE在单任务和多任务设置中均优于竞争基线，同时使用显著更少的可训练参数。

Conclusion: 频域专家适配是LLM微调的有效且参数高效的范式，FourierMoE通过频域分析和MoE架构的结合，解决了多任务微调中的任务干扰和表示不足问题。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for adapting large language models (LLMs) under constrained computational budgets. However, standard PEFT methods often struggle in multi-task fine-tuning settings, where diverse optimization objectives induce task interference and limited parameter budgets lead to representational deficiency. While recent approaches incorporate mixture-of-experts (MoE) to alleviate these issues, they predominantly operate in the spatial domain, which may introduce structural redundancy and parameter overhead. To overcome these limitations, we reformulate adaptation in the spectral domain. Our spectral analysis reveals that different tasks exhibit distinct frequency energy distributions, and that LLM layers display heterogeneous frequency sensitivities. Motivated by these insights, we propose FourierMoE, which integrates the MoE architecture with the inverse discrete Fourier transform (IDFT) for frequency-aware adaptation. Specifically, FourierMoE employs a frequency-adaptive router to dispatch tokens to experts specialized in distinct frequency bands. Each expert learns a set of conjugate-symmetric complex coefficients, preserving complete phase and amplitude information while theoretically guaranteeing lossless IDFT reconstruction into real-valued spatial weights. Extensive evaluations across 28 benchmarks, multiple model architectures, and scales demonstrate that FourierMoE consistently outperforms competitive baselines in both single-task and multi-task settings while using significantly fewer trainable parameters. These results highlight the promise of spectral-domain expert adaptation as an effective and parameter-efficient paradigm for LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [81] [Bridging Deep Learning and Integer Linear Programming: A Predictive-to-Prescriptive Framework for Supply Chain Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01775)
*Khai Banh Nghiep,Duc Nguyen Minh,Lan Hoang Thi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个结合预测与运营分析的三步框架，用于解决零售需求预测中的季节性、异常波动和噪声问题，最终通过优化模型生成成本最优的配送计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售数据常呈现不可调和的季节性、不规则峰值和噪声，使得精确预测几乎无法实现，这对供应链规划中的需求预测提出了重大挑战。

Method: 采用三步分析框架：1) 对180,519笔交易数据进行探索性数据分析，识别长期趋势、季节性和配送相关属性；2) 比较统计时间序列分解模型N-BEATS MSTL与深度学习架构N-HiTS的预测性能；3) 选择最优模型进行未来4周预测，并结合确定性整数线性规划优化配送计划。

Result: N-HiTS和N-BEATS在预测性能上大幅超越统计基准，其中N-BEATS被选为最优模型（预测误差最低）。模型预测未来4周需求为1918单位，通过整数线性规划生成了可行且成本最优的配送计划，满足预算、容量和服务约束。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了精确预测与简单、高度可解释的模型优化在物流实践中的实际影响，为供应链规划提供了有效的分析框架。

Abstract: Although demand forecasting is a critical component of supply chain planning, actual retail data can exhibit irreconcilable seasonality, irregular spikes, and noise, rendering precise projections nearly unattainable. This paper proposes a three-step analytical framework that combines forecasting and operational analytics. The first stage consists of exploratory data analysis, where delivery-tracked data from 180,519 transactions are partitioned, and long-term trends, seasonality, and delivery-related attributes are examined. Secondly, the forecasting performance of a statistical time series decomposition model N-BEATS MSTL and a recent deep learning architecture N-HiTS were compared. N-BEATS and N-HiTS were both statistically, and hence were N-BEATS's and N-HiTS's statistically selected. Most recent time series deep learning models, N-HiTS, N-BEATS. N-HiTS and N-BEATS N-HiTS and N-HiTS outperformed the statistical benchmark to a large extent. N-BEATS was selected to be the most optimized model, as the one with the lowest forecasting error, in the 3rd and final stage forecasting values of the next 4 weeks of 1918 units, and provided those as a model with a set of deterministically integer linear program outcomes that are aimed to minimize the total delivery time with a set of bound budget, capacity, and service constraints. The solution allocation provided a feasible and cost-optimal shipping plan. Overall, the study provides a compelling example of the practical impact of precise forecasting and simple, highly interpretable model optimization in logistics.

</details>


### [82] [Graph Neural Operator Towards Edge Deployability and Portability for Sparse-to-Dense, Real-Time Virtual Sensing on Irregular Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01802)
*William Howes,Jason Yoo,Kazuma Kobayashi,Subhankar Sarkar,Farid Ahmed,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VIRSO是一种基于图的神经算子，用于不规则几何上的稀疏到密集重建，结合可变连接性算法V-KNN进行网格感知图构建，在边缘设备上实现实时虚拟传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中密集仪器部署成本高、不可行，传统物理求解器计算延迟和功耗大，无法在资源受限的监控系统中实时使用，需要一种能在边缘设备上高效运行的虚拟传感方法。

Method: 提出VIRSO图神经算子进行稀疏到密集重建，采用V-KNN算法构建网格感知图，结合谱分析和空间分析，将推理重新定义为测量过程，降低延迟和功耗。

Result: 在三个核热工水力基准测试中，重建比47:1到156:1，平均相对L2误差低于1%，参数更少；10层配置将EDP从206J·ms降至10.1J·ms；在Jetson Orin Nano上功耗低于10W，延迟低于1秒。

Conclusion: VIRSO证明了边缘可行性和硬件可移植性，提出了计算感知算子学习作为资源受限环境中实时传感的新范式。

Abstract: Accurate sensing of spatially distributed physical fields typically requires dense instrumentation, which is often infeasible in real-world systems due to cost, accessibility, and environmental constraints. Physics-based solvers address this through direct numerical integration of governing equations, but their computational latency and power requirements preclude real-time use in resource-constrained monitoring and control systems. Here we introduce VIRSO (Virtual Irregular Real-Time Sparse Operator), a graph-based neural operator for sparse-to-dense reconstruction on irregular geometries, and a variable-connectivity algorithm, Variable KNN (V-KNN), for mesh-informed graph construction. Unlike prior neural operators that treat hardware deployability as secondary, VIRSO reframes inference as measurement: the combination of both spectral and spatial analysis provides accurate reconstruction without the high latency and power consumption of previous graph-based methodologies with poor scalability, presenting VIRSO as a potential candidate for edge-constrained, real-time virtual sensing. We evaluate VIRSO on three nuclear thermal-hydraulic benchmarks of increasing geometric and multiphysics complexity, across reconstruction ratios from 47:1 to 156:1. VIRSO achieves mean relative $L_2$ errors below 1%, outperforming other benchmark operators while using fewer parameters. The full 10-layer configuration reduces the energy-delay product (EDP) from ${\approx}206$ J$\cdot$ms for the graph operator baseline to $10.1$ J$\cdot$ms on an NVIDIA H200. Implemented on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, all configurations of VIRSO provide sub-10 W power consumption and sub-second latency. These results establish the edge-feasibility and hardware-portability of VIRSO and present compute-aware operator learning as a new paradigm for real-time sensing in inaccessible and resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [83] [CANDI: Curated Test-Time Adaptation for Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01845)
*HyunGi Kim,Jisoo Mok,Hyungyu Lee,Juhyeon Shin,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CANDI提出了一种用于多元时间序列异常检测的测试时自适应框架，通过选择性识别和适应潜在假阳性来应对分布偏移问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中多元时间序列异常检测面临分布偏移问题，导致预训练模型性能严重下降。测试时自适应仅使用未标记测试数据在线更新模型，为解决这一挑战提供了可能

Method: 提出CANDI框架，包含假阳性挖掘策略（基于异常分数和潜在相似性筛选适应样本）和即插即用的时空感知正态性自适应模块，实现结构化模型更新

Result: 实验表明CANDI显著提升了分布偏移下多元时间序列异常检测性能，AUROC提升高达14%，同时使用更少的适应样本

Conclusion: CANDI通过选择性适应策略有效解决了分布偏移问题，在保持预训练知识的同时提高了异常检测的鲁棒性

Abstract: Multivariate time-series anomaly detection (MTSAD) aims to identify deviations from normality in multivariate time-series and is critical in real-world applications. However, in real-world deployments, distribution shifts are ubiquitous and cause severe performance degradation in pre-trained anomaly detector. Test-time adaptation (TTA) updates a pre-trained model on-the-fly using only unlabeled test data, making it promising for addressing this challenge. In this study, we propose CANDI (Curated test-time adaptation for multivariate time-series ANomaly detection under DIstribution shift), a novel TTA framework that selectively identifies and adapts to potential false positives while preserving pre-trained knowledge. CANDI introduces a False Positive Mining (FPM) strategy to curate adaptation samples based on anomaly scores and latent similarity, and incorporates a plug-and-play Spatiotemporally-Aware Normality Adaptation (SANA) module for structurally informed model updates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CANDI significantly improves the performance of MTSAD under distribution shift, improving AUROC up to 14% while using fewer adaptation samples.

</details>


### [84] [Towards Intrinsically Calibrated Uncertainty Quantification in Industrial Data-Driven Models via Diffusion Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01870)
*Yiran Ma,Jerome Le Ny,Zhichao Chen,Zhihuan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散的后验采样框架，用于工业过程数据驱动模型的可靠不确定性量化，无需后处理校准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代过程工业中，数据驱动模型对难以直接测量的关键性能指标进行实时监测至关重要。虽然准确预测很重要，但可靠的不确定性量化对安全、可靠性和决策制定同样关键，而当前数据驱动方法在这方面仍面临重大挑战

Method: 引入基于扩散的后验采样框架，通过忠实后验采样固有地产生良好校准的预测不确定性，无需后处理校准

Result: 在合成分布、基于拉曼的苯乙酸软传感器基准和真实氨合成案例研究中，该方法在不确定性校准和预测准确性方面均优于现有UQ技术

Conclusion: 扩散采样器为工业应用中不确定性感知建模提供了一种原则性且可扩展的范式

Abstract: In modern process industries, data-driven models are important tools for real-time monitoring when key performance indicators are difficult to measure directly. While accurate predictions are essential, reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ) is equally critical for safety, reliability, and decision-making, but remains a major challenge in current data-driven approaches. In this work, we introduce a diffusion-based posterior sampling framework that inherently produces well-calibrated predictive uncertainty via faithful posterior sampling, eliminating the need for post-hoc calibration. In extensive evaluations on synthetic distributions, the Raman-based phenylacetic acid soft sensor benchmark, and a real ammonia synthesis case study, our method achieves practical improvements over existing UQ techniques in both uncertainty calibration and predictive accuracy. These results highlight diffusion samplers as a principled and scalable paradigm for advancing uncertainty-aware modeling in industrial applications.

</details>


### [85] [Robust Graph Representation Learning via Adaptive Spectral Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01878)
*Zhuolong Li,Boxue Yang,Haopeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASPECT框架通过可靠性感知的谱门控机制解决谱图对比学习中的谱困境，在混合图上实现节点级频率自适应融合，超越现有全局谱融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱图对比学习方法在处理同质性和异质性混合图时面临谱困境：高频信号对编码异质性至关重要，但理论分析表明其在谱集中扰动下具有更高方差。全局谱融合策略在具有分离的节点级频率偏好的混合图上被证明是次优的。

Method: 提出ASPECT框架，采用可靠性感知的谱门控机制，通过极小极大博弈实现节点级门控动态重加权频率通道。构建专门针对谱能量分布的对抗器，使用瑞利商惩罚迫使编码器学习既具有结构判别性又具有谱鲁棒性的表示。

Result: 在9个基准测试中的8个上实现了新的最先进性能，有效解耦了有意义的结构异质性与偶然噪声。

Conclusion: ASPECT通过节点级自适应谱融合解决了谱困境，超越了全局谱融合的次优性，为处理混合图提供了一种既鲁棒又具有判别性的谱图对比学习框架。

Abstract: Spectral graph contrastive learning has emerged as a unified paradigm for handling both homophilic and heterophilic graphs by leveraging high-frequency components. However, we identify a fundamental spectral dilemma: while high-frequency signals are indispensable for encoding heterophily, our theoretical analysis proves they exhibit significantly higher variance under spectrally concentrated perturbations. We derive a regret lower bound showing that existing global (node-agnostic) spectral fusion is provably sub-optimal: on mixed graphs with separated node-wise frequency preferences, any global fusion strategy incurs non-vanishing regret relative to a node-wise oracle. To escape this bound, we propose ASPECT, a framework that resolves this dilemma through a reliability-aware spectral gating mechanism. Formulated as a minimax game, ASPECT employs a node-wise gate that dynamically re-weights frequency channels based on their stability against a purpose-built adversary, which explicitly targets spectral energy distributions via a Rayleigh quotient penalty. This design forces the encoder to learn representations that are both structurally discriminative and spectrally robust. Empirical results show that ASPECT achieves new state-of-the-art performance on 8 out of 9 benchmarks, effectively decoupling meaningful structural heterophily from incidental noise.

</details>


### [86] [Enhancing the Reliability of Medical AI through Expert-guided Uncertainty Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01898)
*Aleksei Khalin,Ekaterina Zaychenkova,Aleksandr Yugay,Andrey Goncharov,Sergey Korchagin,Alexey Zaytsev,Egor Ershov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种利用专家意见分歧来估计医疗AI不确定性的新方法，通过双集成模型分别量化认知和随机不确定性，在多个医疗任务中提升不确定性估计质量9%-50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI系统作为第二意见系统可加速工作流程并提高诊断准确性，但AI错误的不可预测性在医疗环境中具有严重后果。现有不确定性估计方法在量化数据模糊性和噪声引起的随机不确定性方面存在局限，需要更有效的方法来构建风险感知的AI系统。

Method: 利用专家响应中的分歧生成训练目标，结合标准数据标签，通过双集成方法（及其轻量变体）基于全方差定律分别估计不确定性的两个组成部分。该方法在二元图像分类、二元和多类图像分割以及多项选择题回答任务中进行了验证。

Result: 实验表明，整合专家知识可将不确定性估计质量提升9%至50%，具体取决于任务类型。该方法在医疗应用中为构建风险感知AI系统提供了宝贵的信息源。

Conclusion: 通过利用专家分歧来训练模型，可以显著改进医疗AI系统中的不确定性估计，特别是随机不确定性的量化，从而增强系统的风险感知能力，为高风险病例提供更好的安全保障。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems accelerate medical workflows and improve diagnostic accuracy in healthcare, serving as second-opinion systems. However, the unpredictability of AI errors poses a significant challenge, particularly in healthcare contexts, where mistakes can have severe consequences. A widely adopted safeguard is to pair predictions with uncertainty estimation, enabling human experts to focus on high-risk cases while streamlining routine verification. Current uncertainty estimation methods, however, remain limited, particularly in quantifying aleatoric uncertainty, which arises from data ambiguity and noise. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages disagreement in expert responses to generate targets for training machine learning models. These targets are used in conjunction with standard data labels to estimate two components of uncertainty separately, as given by the law of total variance, via a two-ensemble approach, as well as its lightweight variant. We validate our method on binary image classification, binary and multi-class image segmentation, and multiple-choice question answering. Our experiments demonstrate that incorporating expert knowledge can enhance uncertainty estimation quality by $9\%$ to $50\%$ depending on the task, making this source of information invaluable for the construction of risk-aware AI systems in healthcare applications.

</details>


### [87] [annbatch unlocks terabyte-scale training of biological data in anndata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01949)
*Ilan Gold,Felix Fischer,Lucas Arnoldt,F. Alexander Wolf,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: annbatch是一个专为anndata设计的mini-batch加载器，支持直接在磁盘数据集上进行out-of-core训练，解决了生物数据规模超过系统内存时的数据访问瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物数据集的规模已常规性地超过系统内存，使得数据访问而非模型计算成为训练机器学习模型的主要瓶颈。这一瓶颈在生物学中尤为突出，因为广泛使用的社区数据格式需要支持异构元数据、稀疏和密集检测以及现有计算生态系统中的下游分析。

Method: 开发annbatch，这是一个原生集成到anndata中的mini-batch加载器，能够直接在磁盘支持的数据集上进行out-of-core训练。它保持了与scverse生态系统的完全兼容性。

Result: 在单细胞转录组学、显微镜和全基因组测序基准测试中，annbatch将加载吞吐量提高了高达一个数量级，并将训练时间从数天缩短到数小时。

Conclusion: annbatch为可扩展的生物AI建立了实用的数据加载基础设施，允许在不放弃标准生物数据格式的情况下使用日益庞大和多样化的数据集。

Abstract: The scale of biological datasets now routinely exceeds system memory, making data access rather than model computation the primary bottleneck in training machine-learning models. This bottleneck is particularly acute in biology, where widely used community data formats must support heterogeneous metadata, sparse and dense assays, and downstream analysis within established computational ecosystems. Here we present annbatch, a mini-batch loader native to anndata that enables out-of-core training directly on disk-backed datasets. Across single-cell transcriptomics, microscopy and whole-genome sequencing benchmarks, annbatch increases loading throughput by up to an order of magnitude and shortens training from days to hours, while remaining fully compatible with the scverse ecosystem. Annbatch establishes a practical data-loading infrastructure for scalable biological AI, allowing increasingly large and diverse datasets to be used without abandoning standard biological data formats. Github: https://github.com/scverse/annbatch

</details>


### [88] [Learn by Surprise, Commit by Proof](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01951)
*Kang-Sin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LSCP是一种自门控后训练框架，通过异常高损失检测、自问答验证和基于置信度的β₂调整，实现自主知识获取，防止死记硬背并保护相邻知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统微调导致的死记硬背问题，同时防止新知识污染已有知识。模型需要自主识别知识缺口，并以生物学记忆巩固的方式将上下文信息选择性固化到参数权重中。

Method: 当段落产生异常高每词元损失时，LSCP标记该段落，生成问答链迫使模型表达自身知识并识别缺口，然后根据置信深度k（段落通过的自验证步骤数）按β₂=0.999·r^k比例调整AdamW的β₂参数。整个学习强度由单一参数r控制。

Result: 标准微调产生死记硬背（扰动间隙11.6±0.2倍基线），而所有LSCP条件学习语义（2.7-3.0倍）。r=1.0条件（相同优化器、几乎相同数据，仅问答格式不同）确认训练数据格式是防止记忆的主要机制；门控保护相邻知识免受污染内容影响（r=0.98时相邻问题准确率93±7% vs 90%基线）。

Conclusion: LSCP框架通过自门控机制实现自主知识获取，防止死记硬背，保护已有知识，并模拟生物记忆巩固过程。训练数据格式而非β₂门控是防止记忆的主要因素，门控主要保护相邻知识免受污染。

Abstract: We propose LSCP, a self-gated post-training framework for autonomous knowledge acquisition: learning only what a model does not already know, verified against what it does know, at a strength proportional to conviction, with no external oracle. When a passage produces anomalously high per-token loss, LSCP flags it, generates a Q&A chain that forces the model to articulate its own knowledge and identify gaps, then adjusts AdamW's $β_2$ proportionally to conviction depth k (the number of self-verification steps the passage survives) via $β_2 = 0.999 \cdot r^k$. The entire learning intensity is governed by a single parameter $r$. Beyond new knowledge, this process sharpens weakly encoded existing knowledge, which is a primary source of hallucination. The framework is self-extinguishing: as the model learns, per-token loss on learned passages decreases toward the surprisal threshold and the system progressively converges to standard AdamW. This models biological memory consolidation: temporary information in the context window is selectively consolidated into parametric weights, the model's long-term memory. Experiments on the reference model (Qwen3-14B) and across six models (8B--32B, four families) show that standard fine-tuning produces rote memorization (perturbation gap (the ratio of paraphrase to original perplexity) of 11.6 +- 0.2 x baseline) while all LSCP conditions learn semantically (2.7--3.0x). The r=1.0 condition (identical optimizer, nearly identical data, only Q&A format differs) confirms that the training data format, not $β_2$ gating, is the primary mechanism preventing memorization; gating instead protects neighboring knowledge from contamination by corrupt content (93 +- 7% accuracy on adjacent questions at r=0.98 vs. 90% baseline).

</details>


### [89] [World Action Verifier: Self-Improving World Models via Forward-Inverse Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01985)
*Yuejiang Liu,Fan Feng,Lingjing Kong,Weifeng Lu,Jinzhou Tang,Kun Zhang,Kevin Murphy,Chelsea Finn,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAV框架通过将动作条件状态预测分解为状态合理性和动作可达性两个因素，并分别验证，使世界模型能够识别自身预测错误并进行自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用世界模型需要比策略学习更广泛的鲁棒性，必须对大量次优动作保持可靠预测，而这些动作在动作标记的交互数据中往往覆盖不足。

Method: 将动作条件状态预测分解为状态合理性和动作可达性；利用视频语料库生成多样化子目标；使用稀疏逆模型从状态特征子集推断动作；通过生成子目标、推断动作和前向推演的循环一致性提供验证机制。

Result: 在MiniGrid、RoboMimic和ManiSkill的九个任务中，方法实现了2倍的样本效率提升，下游策略性能提高了18%。

Conclusion: WAV框架通过利用动作自由数据的广泛可用性和动作相关特征的低维度性这两个不对称性，为世界模型提供了有效的自我验证机制，在探索不足的区域显著提升了性能。

Abstract: General-purpose world models promise scalable policy evaluation, optimization, and planning, yet achieving the required level of robustness remains challenging. Unlike policy learning, which primarily focuses on optimal actions, a world model must be reliable over a much broader range of suboptimal actions, which are often insufficiently covered by action-labeled interaction data. To address this challenge, we propose World Action Verifier (WAV), a framework that enables world models to identify their own prediction errors and self-improve. The key idea is to decompose action-conditioned state prediction into two factors -- state plausibility and action reachability -- and verify each separately. We show that these verification problems can be substantially easier than predicting future states due to two underlying asymmetries: the broader availability of action-free data and the lower dimensionality of action-relevant features. Leveraging these asymmetries, we augment a world model with (i) a diverse subgoal generator obtained from video corpora and (ii) a sparse inverse model that infers actions from a subset of state features. By enforcing cycle consistency among generated subgoals, inferred actions, and forward rollouts, WAV provides an effective verification mechanism in under-explored regimes, where existing methods typically fail. Across nine tasks spanning MiniGrid, RoboMimic, and ManiSkill, our method achieves 2x higher sample efficiency while improving downstream policy performance by 18%.

</details>


### [90] [Apriel-Reasoner: RL Post-Training for General-Purpose and Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02007)
*Rafael Pardinas,Ehsan Kamalloo,David Vazquez,Alexandre Drouin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Apriel-Reasoner是一个15B参数的开源LLM，通过多领域RL后训练方法在数学、代码生成、指令遵循、逻辑谜题和函数调用五个领域进行训练，采用自适应领域采样和难度感知长度惩罚机制，在保持推理准确性的同时显著缩短推理轨迹长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿开源模型使用跨领域RLVR训练，但训练配方和领域混合通常不公开。跨领域联合优化面临挑战：领域间在轨迹长度、问题难度和样本效率上差异巨大。长思维链推理会增加推理成本和延迟，因此效率对实际部署至关重要。

Method: 1) 在Apriel-Base 15B参数模型上进行多领域RL后训练，使用五个公开数据集；2) 引入自适应领域采样机制，在异构轨迹动态中保持目标领域比例；3) 提出难度感知的标准长度惩罚扩展，无需额外训练开销，鼓励困难问题使用更长推理，简单问题使用更短轨迹；4) 训练时严格限制16K token输出预算，推理时扩展到32K tokens。

Result: Apriel-Reasoner在AIME 2025、GPQA、MMLU-Pro和LiveCodeBench上优于Apriel-Base，同时产生30-50%更短的推理轨迹。在相似规模的开源模型中达到同等性能，但token成本更低，从而推进了准确性与token预算的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: Apriel-Reasoner通过可复现的多领域RL后训练配方，结合自适应领域采样和难度感知长度惩罚，实现了在保持推理准确性的同时显著提高效率，为实际部署提供了更优的准确性与token预算权衡。

Abstract: Building general-purpose reasoning models using reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) across diverse domains has been widely adopted by frontier open-weight models. However, their training recipes and domain mixtures are often not disclosed. Joint optimization across domains poses significant challenges: domains vary widely in rollout length, problem difficulty and sample efficiency. Further, models with long chain-of-thought traces increase inference cost and latency, making efficiency critical for practical deployment. We present Apriel-Reasoner, trained with a fully reproducible multi-domain RL post-training recipe on Apriel-Base, a 15B-parameter open-weight LLM, across five domains using public datasets: mathematics, code generation, instruction following, logical puzzles and function calling. We introduce an adaptive domain sampling mechanism that preserves target domain ratios despite heterogeneous rollout dynamics, and a difficulty-aware extension of the standard length penalty that, with no additional training overhead, encourages longer reasoning for difficult problems and shorter traces for easy ones. Trained with a strict 16K-token output budget, Apriel-Reasoner generalizes to 32K tokens at inference and improves over Apriel-Base on AIME 2025, GPQA, MMLU-Pro, and LiveCodeBench while producing 30-50% shorter reasoning traces. It matches strong open-weight models of similar size at lower token cost, thereby pushing the Pareto frontier of accuracy versus token budget.

</details>


### [91] [Feature Weighting Improves Pool-Based Sequential Active Learning for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02019)
*Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种特征加权的主动学习回归方法，通过在样本间距离计算中引入特征权重来改进样本选择效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动学习回归方法在计算样本间距离时未考虑不同特征的重要性，导致样本选择效果不理想。代表性（representativeness）和多样性（diversity）是主动学习中的关键考虑因素，但现有方法在距离计算中缺乏特征权重机制。

Method: 提出了三种特征加权的单任务主动学习回归方法和两种特征加权的多任务主动学习回归方法。核心思想是利用从少量已标记样本训练得到的岭回归系数作为特征权重，在样本间距离计算中对不同特征进行加权。

Result: 实验表明，这种易于实现的增强方法几乎总是能提升四种现有主动学习回归方法的性能，在单任务和多任务回归问题中均有效。

Conclusion: 特征加权策略能显著改进主动学习回归的样本选择效果，且该方法易于实现，还可扩展到流式主动学习和分类算法中。

Abstract: Pool-based sequential active learning for regression (ALR) optimally selects a small number of samples sequentially from a large pool of unlabeled samples to label, so that a more accurate regression model can be constructed under a given labeling budget. Representativeness and diversity, which involve computing the distances among different samples, are important considerations in ALR. However, previous ALR approaches do not incorporate the importance of different features in inter-sample distance computation, resulting in sub-optimal sample selection. This paper proposes three feature weighted single-task ALR approaches and two feature weighted multi-task ALR approaches, where the ridge regression coefficients trained from a small amount of previously labeled samples are used to weight the corresponding features in inter-sample distance computation. Experiments showed that this easy-to-implement enhancement almost always improves the performance of four existing ALR approaches, in both single-task and multi-task regression problems. The feature weighting strategy may also be easily extended to stream-based ALR, and classification algorithms.

</details>


### [92] [AA-SVD : Anchored and Adaptive SVD for Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02119)
*Atul Kumar Sinha,François Fleuret*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于低秩分解的快速LLM压缩框架，通过同时考虑原始输入和上游压缩引起的分布偏移，结合端到端块级优化，实现高效的大规模模型压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分解的压缩方法存在两个主要问题：1）仅优化原始输入，忽略上游压缩引起的分布偏移，导致误差传播；2）仅依赖偏移输入，可能偏离原始输出。需要一种能同时考虑两者的压缩方法。

Method: 提出双锚定低秩分解框架：1）同时考虑原始输入和压缩引起的分布偏移；2）在单个层压缩基础上，对每个transformer块进行端到端细化，最小化块级输出失真；3）通过锚定压缩层到原始输出并显式建模输入分布偏移，找到保持功能等效的低秩近似。

Result: 在大语言模型上的实验表明，该方法在各种压缩比下始终优于现有的SVD基线方法，在激进压缩预算下优势更加明显，而竞争方法会显著退化或完全崩溃。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种实用的解决方案，支持高效、大规模模型部署，通过同时考虑原始输入和分布偏移，结合块级端到端优化，实现了更好的压缩效果和功能保持。

Abstract: We introduce a fast low-rank factorization-based framework for compressing large language models that enables rapid compression of billion-parameter models without retraining. Unlike existing factorization-based approaches that optimize only on the original inputs, ignoring distribution shifts from upstream compression and thus propagating errors forward, or those that rely only on shifted inputs and risk drifting away from the original outputs, our approach accounts for both. Beyond individual layer compression, we further refine each transformer block end-to-end, minimizing block-level output distortion and allowing compressed layers to jointly compensate for accumulated errors. By anchoring each compressed layer to the original outputs while explicitly modeling input distribution shifts, our method finds a low-rank approximation that maintains functional equivalence with the original model. Experiments on large language models show that our method consistently outperforms existing SVD-based baselines across compression ratios, with the advantage becoming increasingly pronounced at aggressive compression budgets, where competing methods degrade substantially or collapse entirely, offering a practical solution for efficient, large-scale model deployment.

</details>


### [93] [Auction-Based Online Policy Adaptation for Evolving Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02151)
*Guruprerana Shabadi,Kaushik Mallik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于拍卖机制的模块化多目标强化学习框架，通过自私局部策略竞标执行权，动态适应运行时目标变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多目标强化学习中目标在运行时动态出现或消失的问题，需要设计能够高效适应目标集合变化的策略

Method: 模块化框架：每个目标对应一个自私局部策略，通过拍卖机制协调；策略竞标执行权，出价反映当前状态紧迫性；最高出价者选择动作；目标变化时只需添加或移除对应策略；使用PPO并发训练策略

Result: 在Atari Assault和基于网格世界的路径规划任务（动态目标）上评估，相比使用PPO训练的单体策略，本方法获得显著更好的性能

Conclusion: 提出的拍卖机制模块化框架能够有效处理运行时目标变化，通过自私策略竞争和协调实现动态权衡，优于传统单体策略方法

Abstract: We consider multi-objective reinforcement learning problems where objectives come from an identical family -- such as the class of reachability objectives -- and may appear or disappear at runtime. Our goal is to design adaptive policies that can efficiently adjust their behaviors as the set of active objectives changes. To solve this problem, we propose a modular framework where each objective is supported by a selfish local policy, and coordination is achieved through a novel auction-based mechanism: policies bid for the right to execute their actions, with bids reflecting the urgency of the current state. The highest bidder selects the action, enabling a dynamic and interpretable trade-off among objectives. Going back to the original adaptation problem, when objectives change, the system adapts by simply adding or removing the corresponding policies. Moreover, as objectives arise from the same family, identical copies of a parameterized policy can be deployed, facilitating immediate adaptation at runtime. We show how the selfish local policies can be computed by turning the problem into a general-sum game, where the policies compete against each other to fulfill their own objectives. To succeed, each policy must not only optimize its own objective, but also reason about the presence of other goals and learn to produce calibrated bids that reflect relative priority. In our implementation, the policies are trained concurrently using proximal policy optimization (PPO). We evaluate on Atari Assault and a gridworld-based path-planning task with dynamic targets. Our method achieves substantially better performance than monolithic policies trained with PPO.

</details>


### [94] [Smoothing the Landscape: Causal Structure Learning via Diffusion Denoising Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02250)
*Hao Zhu,Di Zhou,Donna Slonim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DDCD利用扩散模型的去噪分数匹配目标来平滑梯度，实现更快、更稳定的收敛，并提出自适应k跳无环约束来改进运行时间，用于从观测数据中发现因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（如NOTEARS和DAG-GNN）在高维数据中面临可扩展性和稳定性问题，特别是在特征-样本不平衡的情况下。需要更高效、更稳定的方法来从观测数据中发现因果依赖关系。

Method: 提出Denoising Diffusion Causal Discovery (DDCD)框架：1) 利用扩散模型的去噪分数匹配目标平滑梯度，实现更快、更稳定的收敛；2) 提出自适应k跳无环约束，避免矩阵求逆，改进运行时间；3) 与生成式扩散模型不同，DDCD使用反向去噪过程推断参数化的因果结构而非生成数据。

Result: 在合成基准数据上展示了DDCD的竞争性性能，并在两个真实世界示例上进行了定性分析，证明了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: DDCD通过结合扩散模型的去噪分数匹配目标和自适应无环约束，为高维观测数据中的因果发现提供了更高效、更稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Understanding causal dependencies in observational data is critical for informing decision-making. These relationships are often modeled as Bayesian Networks (BNs) and Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs). Existing methods, such as NOTEARS and DAG-GNN, often face issues with scalability and stability in high-dimensional data, especially when there is a feature-sample imbalance. Here, we show that the denoising score matching objective of diffusion models could smooth the gradients for faster, more stable convergence. We also propose an adaptive k-hop acyclicity constraint that improves runtime over existing solutions that require matrix inversion. We name this framework Denoising Diffusion Causal Discovery (DDCD). Unlike generative diffusion models, DDCD utilizes the reverse denoising process to infer a parameterized causal structure rather than to generate data. We demonstrate the competitive performance of DDCDs on synthetic benchmarking data. We also show that our methods are practically useful by conducting qualitative analyses on two real-world examples. Code is available at this url: https://github.com/haozhu233/ddcd.

</details>


### [95] [Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Control under Time-Varying Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02260)
*Klemens Iten,Bruce Lee,Chenhao Li,Lenart Treven,Andreas Krause,Bhavya Sukhija*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究非平稳动态系统中的强化学习控制问题，提出基于高斯过程模型的持续学习算法，通过自适应数据缓冲机制处理动态变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的系统动态通常是非平稳的（由于漂移、磨损或运行条件变化），而传统的基于学习的控制方法假设系统动态是平稳的，这一假设在实际应用中经常被违反。需要研究在时变动态下的强化学习控制问题。

Method: 采用持续模型强化学习框架，使用高斯过程动态模型，在频率主义变化预算假设下进行分析。提出实用的乐观模型强化学习算法，包含自适应数据缓冲机制，限制过时数据的影响以保持校准的不确定性。

Result: 分析表明，持续的非平稳性需要明确限制过时数据的影响，以保持校准的不确定性和有意义的动态遗憾保证。在具有非平稳动态的连续控制基准测试中，所提出的算法表现出改进的性能。

Conclusion: 处理非平稳动态系统需要专门的算法设计，特别是需要机制来管理过时数据的影响。提出的自适应数据缓冲机制结合乐观模型强化学习框架，为时变动态环境中的控制问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning-based control methods typically assume stationary system dynamics, an assumption often violated in real-world systems due to drift, wear, or changing operating conditions. We study reinforcement learning for control under time-varying dynamics. We consider a continual model-based reinforcement learning setting in which an agent repeatedly learns and controls a dynamical system whose transition dynamics evolve across episodes. We analyze the problem using Gaussian process dynamics models under frequentist variation-budget assumptions. Our analysis shows that persistent non-stationarity requires explicitly limiting the influence of outdated data to maintain calibrated uncertainty and meaningful dynamic regret guarantees. Motivated by these insights, we propose a practical optimistic model-based reinforcement learning algorithm with adaptive data buffer mechanisms and demonstrate improved performance on continuous control benchmarks with non-stationary dynamics.

</details>


### [96] [Unifying Group-Relative and Self-Distillation Policy Optimization via Sample Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02288)
*Gengsheng Li,Tianyu Yang,Junfeng Fang,Mingyang Song,Mao Zheng,Haiyun Guo,Dan Zhang,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SRPO统一了GRPO和SDPO的优势，通过样本路由机制将正确样本分配给GRPO进行奖励对齐强化，将失败样本分配给SDPO进行针对性对数级修正，解决了SDPO长期训练不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法存在局限性：GRPO采用粗粒度信用分配，对失败rollout统一惩罚，缺乏针对特定偏差的token级关注；SDPO虽然提供更密集的对数级监督实现快速早期改进，但在长期训练中经常崩溃。需要解决SDPO晚期不稳定的内在缺陷。

Method: 提出样本路由策略优化(SRPO)：统一on-policy框架，将正确样本路由到GRPO的奖励对齐强化学习，失败样本路由到SDPO的针对性对数级修正。引入熵感知动态加权机制，抑制高熵不可靠的蒸馏目标，强调置信度高的目标。

Result: 在五个基准测试和两种模型规模上，SRPO同时实现了SDPO的快速早期改进和GRPO的长期稳定性。在Qwen3-8B上，五个基准测试平均性能比GRPO提高3.4%，比SDPO提高6.3%，同时产生适中的响应长度，每步计算成本降低高达17.2%。

Conclusion: SRPO成功解决了SDPO的晚期不稳定性问题，统一了GRPO和SDPO的优势，实现了快速收敛和长期稳定性的平衡，在多个基准测试上超越了两种基线方法的峰值性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard paradigm for post-training large language models. While Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely adopted, its coarse credit assignment uniformly penalizes failed rollouts, lacking the token-level focus needed to efficiently address specific deviations. Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) addresses this by providing denser, more targeted logit-level supervision that facilitates rapid early improvement, yet it frequently collapses during prolonged training. We trace this late-stage instability to two intrinsic flaws: self-distillation on already-correct samples introduces optimization ambiguity, and the self-teacher's signal reliability progressively degrades. To resolve these issues, we propose Sample-Routed Policy Optimization (SRPO), a unified on-policy framework that routes correct samples to GRPO's reward-aligned reinforcement and failed samples to SDPO's targeted logit-level correction. SRPO further incorporates an entropy-aware dynamic weighting mechanism to suppress high-entropy, unreliable distillation targets while emphasizing confident ones. Evaluated across five benchmarks and two model scales, SRPO achieves both the rapid early improvement of SDPO and the long-horizon stability of GRPO. It consistently surpasses the peak performance of both baselines, raising the five-benchmark average on Qwen3-8B by 3.4% over GRPO and 6.3% over SDPO, while simultaneously yielding moderate response lengths and lowering per-step compute cost by up to 17.2%.

</details>


### [97] [Taming the Exponential: A Fast Softmax Surrogate for Integer-Native Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02292)
*Dimitrios Danopoulos,Enrico Lupi,Michael Kagan,Maurizio Pierini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HCCS（Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax）作为softmax的替代方案，针对AMD Versal AI Engines优化，使用int8运算显著提升小模型低精度推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Transformer的MHA块中，softmax在低精度推理时成为计算瓶颈，特别是小模型中的指数运算和归一化开销大。AMD AI Engines现有实现使用bfloat16或LUT进行指数运算，限制了吞吐量且未能充分利用整数向量处理单元。

Method: 提出HCCS：使用基于最大中心化注意力logits的裁剪线性映射作为指数softmax的有界单调替代。包含轻量级校准参数，基于代表性数据集离线优化，为每个注意力头单独校准以保持统计特性。针对AMD AI Engines的int8 MAC单元进行硬件优化实现。

Result: HCCS显著超过其他参考实现的速度性能，在小模型或重度量化MHA工作负载上，经过量化感知重训练后保持竞争力的任务精度。这是首个针对AMD AI Engines的int8优化softmax替代方案。

Conclusion: HCCS有效解决了softmax在低精度推理中的计算瓶颈问题，通过硬件友好的int8实现充分利用AI Engines的计算能力，在保持精度的同时大幅提升推理速度。

Abstract: Softmax can become a computational bottleneck in the Transformer model's Multi-Head Attention (MHA) block, particularly in small models under low-precision inference, where exponentiation and normalization incur significant overhead. As such, we suggest using Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax (HCCS), a bounded, monotone surrogate to the exponential softmax function, which uses a clipped linear mapping of the max centered attention logits. This approximation produces a stable probability distribution, maintains the ordering of the original logits and has non-negative values. HCCS differs from previous softmax surrogates as it includes a set of lightweight calibration parameters that are optimized offline based on a representative dataset and calibrated for each individual attention head to preserve the statistical properties of the individual heads. We describe a hardware-motivated implementation of HCCS for high-throughput scenarios targeting the AMD Versal AI Engines. The current reference implementations from AMD for this platform rely upon either bfloat16 arithmetic or LUTs to perform the exponential operation, which might limit the throughput of the platform and fail to utilize the high-throughput integer vector processing units of the AI Engine. In contrast, HCCS provides a natural mapping to the AI Engines' int8 multiply accumulate (MAC) units. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first int8 optimized softmax surrogate for AMD AI engines that significantly exceeds the speed performance of other reference implementations while maintaining competitive task accuracy on small or heavily quantized MHA workloads after quantization-aware retraining.

</details>


### [98] [go-$m$HC: Direct Parameterization of Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections via Generalized Orthostochastic Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02309)
*Torque Dandachi,Sophia Diggs-Galligan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出go-mHC方法，通过广义正交随机矩阵理论实现双随机矩阵的精确参数化，以O(d³)复杂度在计算效率与表达能力之间连续插值，显著提升流混合任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双随机矩阵能够实现残差流之间的学习混合，但精确高效地参数化双随机矩阵集合（Birkhoff多面体）仍是一个开放挑战。现有精确方法随流数量d呈阶乘级增长，而Kronecker分解方法虽高效但表达能力有限。

Method: 基于广义正交随机矩阵理论提出新颖的精确参数化方法，复杂度为O(d³)，通过单个超参数s在计算高效边界与完全表达能力的Birkhoff多面体之间连续插值。该方法在流形约束超连接(mHC)框架基础上实例化为go-mHC，可与Kronecker分解方法自然组合，以相似的FLOP成本大幅恢复表达能力。

Result: 谱分析表明go-mHC比Kronecker分解基线更完整地填充Birkhoff多面体。在合成流混合任务中，go-mHC达到最小理论损失且收敛速度提升高达10倍。在3000万参数的GPT风格语言模型中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: go-mHC的表达能力、效率和精确性为将d作为模型容量的新维度进行扩展提供了实用途径，解决了双随机矩阵参数化的核心挑战。

Abstract: Doubly stochastic matrices enable learned mixing across residual streams, but parameterizing the set of doubly stochastic matrices (the Birkhoff polytope) exactly and efficiently remains an open challenge. Existing exact methods scale factorially with the number of streams ($d$), while Kronecker-factorized approaches are efficient but expressivity-limited. We introduce a novel exact parameterization grounded in the theory of generalized orthostochastic matrices, which scales as $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$ and exposes a single hyperparameter $s$ which continuously interpolates between a computationally efficient boundary and the fully expressive Birkhoff polytope. Building on Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections ($m$HC), a framework for learned dynamic layer connectivity, we instantiate this parameterization in go-$m$HC. Our method composes naturally with Kronecker-factorized methods, substantially recovering expressivity at similar FLOP costs. Spectral analysis indicates that go-$m$HC fills the Birkhoff polytope far more completely than Kronecker-factorized baselines. On synthetic stream-mixing tasks, go-$m$HC achieves the minimum theoretical loss while converging up to $10\times$ faster. We validate our approach in a 30M parameter GPT-style language model. The expressivity, efficiency, and exactness of go-$m$HC offer a practical avenue for scaling $d$ as a new dimension of model capacity.

</details>


### [99] [Batched Contextual Reinforcement: A Task-Scaling Law for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02322)
*Bangji Yang,Hongbo Ma,Jiajun Fan,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BCR是一种单阶段训练范式，通过在共享上下文窗口中同时解决N个问题来解锁高效推理，减少token消耗的同时保持或提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链推理方法token消耗过大导致推理成本高昂，而现有的效率方法（如显式长度惩罚、难度估计或多阶段课程）要么降低推理质量，要么需要复杂的训练流程。

Method: 提出批量上下文强化（BCR），这是一种简约的单阶段训练范式，通过简单的结构修改：训练模型在共享上下文窗口中同时解决N个问题，仅通过每个实例的准确性获得奖励。这种表述创建了一个隐式的token预算。

Result: (1) 发现新的任务缩放定律：推理时并发问题数N增加，每个问题的token使用量单调减少，准确性下降远低于基线；(2) BCR挑战传统准确性-效率权衡，在标准单问题推理中实现"免费午餐"现象，在1.5B和4B模型上减少15.8%-62.6% token使用同时保持或提升准确性；(3) 模型自主消除冗余元认知循环；(4) 隐式预算约束成功规避显式长度惩罚的对抗梯度和灾难性优化崩溃。

Conclusion: BCR证明简单的结构激励可以解锁LLM中的潜在高密度推理能力，提供了一种高度稳定的、基于约束的长度控制替代方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models employing Chain-of-Thought reasoning achieve strong performance but suffer from excessive token consumption that inflates inference costs. Existing efficiency methods such as explicit length penalties, difficulty estimators, or multi-stage curricula either degrade reasoning quality or require complex training pipelines. We introduce Batched Contextual Reinforcement, a minimalist, single-stage training paradigm that unlocks efficient reasoning through a simple structural modification: training the model to solve N problems simultaneously within a shared context window, rewarded purely by per-instance accuracy. This formulation creates an implicit token budget that yields several key findings: (1) We identify a novel task-scaling law: as the number of concurrent problems N increases during inference, per-problem token usage decreases monotonically while accuracy degrades far more gracefully than baselines, establishing N as a controllable throughput dimension. (2) BCR challenges the traditional accuracy-efficiency trade-off by demonstrating a "free lunch" phenomenon at standard single-problem inference. Across both 1.5B and 4B model families, BCR reduces token usage by 15.8% to 62.6% while consistently maintaining or improving accuracy across five major mathematical benchmarks. (3) Qualitative analyses reveal emergent self-regulated efficiency, where models autonomously eliminate redundant metacognitive loops without explicit length supervision. (4) Crucially, we empirically demonstrate that implicit budget constraints successfully circumvent the adversarial gradients and catastrophic optimization collapse inherent to explicit length penalties, offering a highly stable, constraint-based alternative for length control. These results prove BCR practical, showing simple structural incentives unlock latent high-density reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Large-Scale Resilience Planning for Wildfire-Prone Electricity-System via Adaptive Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01232)
*Shuyi Chen,Shixiang Zhu,Ramteen Sioshansi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 联合优化电网基础设施配置与运营策略的野火韧性规划框架，通过三级优化模型协调长期基础设施投资与短期运营响应，在不确定野火风险下平衡安全与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力公司面临日益增长的野火风险挑战：输电线故障可能引发野火，而大型火灾会破坏电网运营。当前运营干预措施（如公共安全断电PSPS和快速跳闸保护）若不加区分地部署，可能导致大规模服务中断。基础设施规划决策（如馈线分段和保护配置）对于这些干预措施的有效针对性至关重要。

Method: 开发野火韧性规划框架，联合优化长期基础设施配置和短期运营响应。采用三级优化模型捕捉基础设施规划、野火风险实现和自适应运营决策之间的相互作用。构建数据驱动的不确定性集合，结合分段级预测区间和组级不确定性预算。利用模型结构将问题重构为可处理的鲁棒优化模型，并开发可扩展的列与约束生成算法。

Result: 合成实验和针对投资者所有公用事业配电系统的大规模案例研究表明，分段化和运营缓解策略的协调规划可以显著降低野火风险，同时保持服务可靠性。

Conclusion: 提出的规划框架通过联合优化基础设施配置和运营响应，为电力公司提供了在不确定野火风险下平衡安全与可靠性的有效方法。协调规划方法能够显著降低野火风险，同时最小化服务中断影响。

Abstract: Wildfire risk poses a growing challenge for electric utilities, as powerline failures can ignite wildfires while large fires can disrupt grid operations. Utilities increasingly rely on operational interventions such as Public Safety Power Shutoffs (PSPS) and fast-trip protection to mitigate ignition risk, but these measures can cause widespread service disruptions if deployed indiscriminately. Infrastructure planning decisions--such as feeder sectionalization and protection configuration--play a key role in determining how effectively these interventions can be targeted. We develop a planning framework for wildfire resilience that jointly optimizes long-term infrastructure configuration and short-term operational response under uncertain ignition risk. The problem is formulated as a tri-level optimization model capturing the interaction between infrastructure planning, wildfire risk realization, and adaptive operational decisions. To represent system-wide ignition uncertainty, we construct a data-driven uncertainty set that combines segment-level prediction intervals with group-level uncertainty budgets. Leveraging the model structure, we reformulate the problem as a tractable robust optimization model and develop a scalable column-and-constraint generation algorithm. Synthetic experiments and a large-scale case study on an investor-owned utility distribution system show that coordinated planning of sectionalization and operational mitigation strategies can substantially reduce wildfire risk while maintaining service reliability.

</details>


### [101] [On the mean-variance problem through the lens of multivariate fake stationary affine Volterra dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01300)
*Emmanuel Gnabeyeu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 在假平稳仿射Volterra模型框架下，通过Riccati BSDE方法解决非马尔可夫、非半鞅市场的连续时间Markowitz均值-方差投资组合选择问题，获得最优策略和有效前沿的解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机控制方法无法直接应用于具有无界随机系数的非马尔可夫、非半鞅市场框架下的投资组合优化问题，需要新的数学工具来处理这类复杂市场模型。

Method: 使用Riccati向后随机微分方程(BSDE)的随机因子解法，通过多维Riccati-Volterra方程获得显式解，从而表征最优反馈控制。

Result: 获得了最优投资组合策略和均值-方差有效前沿的解析闭式表达式，这些表达式依赖于相关多元Riccati-Volterra系统的解。通过二维假平稳粗糙Heston模型的数值实验展示了粗糙波动率和随机相关性对最优Markowitz策略的影响。

Conclusion: 在假平稳仿射Volterra模型框架下，Riccati BSDE方法成功解决了非马尔可夫、非半鞅市场的连续时间Markowitz投资组合选择问题，为复杂市场环境下的投资决策提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We investigate the continuous-time Markowitz mean-variance portfolio selection problem within a multivariate class of fake stationary affine Volterra models. In this non-Markovian and non-semimartingale market framework with unbounded random coefficients, the classical stochastic control approach cannot be directly applied to the associated optimization task. Instead, the problem is tackled using a stochastic factor solution to a Riccati backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE). The optimal feedback control is characterized by means of this equation, whose explicit solutions is derived in terms of multi-dimensional Riccati-Volterra equations. Specifically, we obtain analytical closed-form expressions for the optimal portfolio policies as well as the mean-variance efficient frontier, both of which depend on the solution to the associated multivariate Riccati-Volterra system. To illustrate our results, numerical experiments based on a two dimensional fake stationary rough Heston model highlight the impact of rough volatilities and stochastic correlations on the optimal Markowitz strategies.

</details>


### [102] [Risk Control of Traffic Flow Through Chance Constraints and Large Deviation Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01321)
*Rui Xu,Shanyin Tong,Xuan Di*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于大偏差理论的罕见机会约束最优控制框架，用于自主交通管理，解决传统方法难以严格约束安全关键极端事件的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宏观交通控制方法在随机扰动下难以严格约束罕见但安全关键的极端事件，需要开发能够有效处理概率安全约束的控制框架。

Method: 利用大偏差理论（LDT）近似方法，将高度非凸、采样密集的优化问题转化为可处理的确定性非线性规划问题，实现计算可扩展性。

Result: 提出的LDT重构方法在计算可扩展性上表现优异，计算负担不随目标违反概率水平变化，有效避免了传统采样方法的极端扩展瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该框架在实现精确近目标概率控制和计算效率方面优于风险规避基线方法，通过多种交通风险度量的数值模拟验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Existing macroscopic traffic control methods often struggle to strictly regulate rare, safety-critical extreme events under stochastic disturbances. In this paper, we develop a rare chance-constrained optimal control framework for autonomous traffic management. To efficiently enforce these probabilistic safety specifications, we exploit a large deviation theory (LDT) based approximation method, which converts the original highly non-convex, sampling-heavy optimization problem into a tractable deterministic nonlinear programming problem. In addition, the proposed LDT-based reformulation exhibits superior computational scalability, as it maintains a constant computational burden regardless of the target violation probability level, effectively bypassing the extreme scaling bottlenecks of traditional sampling-based methods. The effectiveness of the proposed framework in achieving precise near-target probability control and superior computational efficiency over risk-averse baselines is illustrated through extensive numerical simulations across diverse traffic risk measures.

</details>


### [103] [Output-Feedback Controller Synthesis for Dissipativity and $H_2$ Performance of Autoregressive Systems from Noisy Input-Output Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01347)
*Pietro Kristović,Andrej Jokić,Mircea Lazar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于数据驱动的输出反馈控制器综合方法，用于具有特定自回归形式的离散时间线性时不变系统，目标为实现给定二次供给率的耗散性或特定H₂性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制器设计通常需要精确的系统模型，但在实际应用中，系统模型往往是未知的或难以准确获取。本文旨在开发一种仅利用输入输出数据而不需要完整系统模型知识的控制器综合方法，以应对实际工程中模型不确定性带来的挑战。

Method: 针对离散时间线性时不变系统，采用特定自回归形式表示。利用受控输入和输出的记录轨迹（可能受到未知但有界扰动的影响），通过线性矩阵不等式（LMI）形式推导控制器综合方法。该方法仅假设扰动项已知，而系统模型未知。

Result: 提出的控制器综合方法以线性矩阵不等式形式呈现，在所考虑的问题设定下具有非保守性。该方法能够实现两种控制目标：1）针对给定二次供给率实现耗散性；2）达到指定的H₂性能水平。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了一种数据驱动的输出反馈控制器综合框架，该框架仅依赖输入输出数据而不需要完整的系统模型知识，为处理模型不确定性提供了有效的解决方案，并在理论保证下实现了非保守的控制性能。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a data-driven output-feedback controller synthesis method for discrete-time linear time-invariant systems in a specific autoregressive form. The synthesis goal is either to achieve dissipativity with respect to a given quadratic supply rate, or to achieve given $H_2$ performance level. It is assumed that the model of the plant is unknown, except for the disturbance term. To compensate for the lack of model knowledge, we have a recorded trajectory of the controlled input and the output available for control, which can be corrupted by an unknown but bounded disturbance. Derived controller synthesis method is in the form of linear matrix inequalities and is nonconservative within the considered problem setting.

</details>


### [104] [Sterile mosquito release via intelligent proportional controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01355)
*Cédric Join,Luis Almeida,Michel Fliess*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究应用模型无关控制、超局部模型和智能比例控制器来解决昆虫不育技术中的种群控制问题，重点关注采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 昆虫不育技术（SIT）是一种通过释放不育雄性昆虫来控制害虫和病媒昆虫种群的方法。然而，这种复杂的控制问题需要有效的控制策略，特别是采样策略方面存在挑战。

Method: 采用模型无关控制、超局部模型和智能比例控制器方法，这些方法已在多个领域证明有效，能够处理SIT中的复杂控制问题，特别是采样策略。

Result: 通过计算机模拟展示了所提方法的有效性，并进行了讨论分析。

Conclusion: 模型无关控制、超局部模型和智能比例控制器为昆虫不育技术中的种群控制问题提供了有效的解决方案，特别是能够解决关键的采样问题。

Abstract: The Sterile Insect Technique (SIT) against insect pests and insect vectors consists of releasing males that have been previously sterilized in order to reduce or eliminate a specific wild population. We study this complex control question via model-free control, ultra-local models, and intelligent proportional controllers that have already proven their effectiveness in various fields. They permit addressing, perhaps for the first time, the essential sampling question. Computer simulations are displayed and discussed.

</details>


### [105] [Exponential Stabilization of Moving Shockwave in ARZ Traffic Model via Boundary Control: Explicit Gains and Arbitrary Decay Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01382)
*Mina Cao,Mamadou Diagne,Peipei Shang,Lei Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为ARZ交通流模型开发边界反馈控制，将移动边界双曲系统转换为固定域上的无激波增广系统，通过基于激波位置的移动坐标和修正Lyapunov泛函实现任意指数衰减率的激波位置和系统状态稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通拥堵激波会严重影响交通流效率，需要设计有效的控制策略来稳定激波平衡状态。现有研究主要关注无激波系统的稳定性，而对包含激波移动边界的交通流模型的稳定控制研究较少。

Method: 1. 将移动边界的2×2双曲系统（分别对应自由流和拥堵流区域）通过基于激波位置的移动坐标转换为固定域上的无激波4×4增广系统；2. 应用修正的Lyapunov泛函处理激波扰动；3. 设计边界反馈控制律。

Result: 证明了在给定反馈控制下，激波位置和系统状态在H^2范数意义下能以任意指数衰减率稳定到预定激波平衡状态。数值模拟验证了稳定化结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 成功开发了ARZ交通流模型中激波平衡的边界反馈控制方法，通过系统变换和Lyapunov分析实现了激波位置和交通状态的指数稳定，为交通拥堵控制提供了理论框架和数值验证。

Abstract: This paper develops boundary feedback controls to stabilize traffic congestion toward a predefined shock equilibrium in the Aw-Rascle-Zhang (ARZ) traffic flow model. We transform the corresponding moving-boundary $2\times2$ hyperbolic system, covering free and congested flow regimes, respectively, into a shock-free $4\times4$ augmented system on a fixed domain via shock-location-based moving coordinates. By applying the modified Lyapunov functionals concerning shock perturbation, we show that the shock position and the state of the system in $H^2$-norm can be stabilized with an arbitrary exponential decay rate via the given feedback controls. Finally, the stabilization results are demonstrated by numerical simulations.

</details>


### [106] [Joint Pricing and Innovation Control in Regulated Recycling-Rate Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01402)
*Bowen Xie,Yijin Gao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受监管的随机扩散模型来刻画回收率，并通过回收投资和产品定价的动态过程，构建了一个生产与工艺创新的联合控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在绿色经济监管框架下，系统管理者需要在产品定价决策与投资支出之间进行权衡。回收率作为受监管的状态过程，引入了额外的政策驱动成本成分，需要建立相应的随机控制模型来优化生产与工艺创新决策。

Method: 构建了受监管的随机扩散模型来描述回收率动态，建立了无限时域折现成本结构下的联合随机控制问题。通过引入Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，并严格证明HJB解与底层控制问题价值函数之间的对应关系，解决了联合受监管随机生产与工艺创新准入控制问题。

Result: 在系统参数的温和、实践导向假设下分析了HJB方程。通过数值实验和敏感性分析，展示了HJB表征的可处理性，并评估了所施加参数条件的实际相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究为绿色经济监管下的生产与工艺创新决策提供了理论框架和数值方法，证明了HJB方程在解决此类联合受监管随机控制问题中的有效性，并通过数值实验验证了模型的实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a regulated stochastic diffusion model for the recycling rate and formulate a joint control problem over production and process innovation via the dynamics of recycling investment and product pricing. The resulting stochastic control problem captures the system manager's trade-off between product-price decisions and investment expenditures under an infinite-horizon discounted cost structure. Owing to the recycling-rate specification, we incorporate two regulated state processes, which induce additional policy-driven cost components in the value function consistent with green-economy regulations. We resolve the jointly regulated stochastic production and process-innovation admission control problem by introducing the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation and providing rigorous proofs that establish the correspondence between the HJB solution and the value function of the underlying control problem. The HJB equation is analyzed under mild, practically motivated assumptions on the system parameters. We further present numerical experiments and sensitivity analyses to illustrate the tractability of the HJB characterization and to assess the practical relevance of the imposed parameter conditions.

</details>


### [107] [Concentration of Stochastic System Trajectories with Time-varying Contraction Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01403)
*Zishun Liu,Liqian Ma,Hongzhe Yu,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文建立了时变收缩条件下非线性随机系统的两个集中不等式，通过平均矩生成函数(AMGF)结合增量稳定性分析和鞅方法，分别得到单时间点和轨迹层面的偏差界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性随机系统在时变收缩条件下的集中性质，为随机轨迹与其确定性对应物之间的偏差提供理论保证，这对于随机安全控制等应用具有重要意义。

Method: 提出平均矩生成函数(AMGF)作为关键能量函数，结合增量稳定性分析得到单时间点集中不等式，再结合鞅方法推导轨迹层面的集中不等式，最后综合两者结果改进强收缩系统的轨迹不等式。

Result: 在概率水平1-δ下，推导的集中不等式确保随机轨迹偏差的界限为O(√log(1/δ))，该界限在所给假设下是紧致的，并通过随机安全控制的案例研究验证了结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为非线性随机系统在时变收缩条件下提供了有效的集中不等式理论框架，所得到的偏差界限在理论上是紧致的，为随机控制等应用提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We establish two concentration inequalities for nonlinear stochastic system under time-varying contraction conditions. The key to our approach is an energy function termed Averaged Moment Generating Function (AMGF). By combining it with incremental stability analysis, we develop a concentration inequality that bounds the deviation between the stochastic system state and its deterministic counterpart. As this inequality is restricted to single time instance, we further combine AMGF with martingale-based methods to derive a concentration inequality that bounds the fluctuation of the entire stochastic trajectory. Additionally, by synthesizing the two results, we significantly improve the trajectory-level concentration inequality for strongly contractive systems. Given the probability level $1-δ$, the derived inequalities ensure an $\mO(\sqrt{\log(1/δ))}$ bound on the deviation of stochastic trajectories, which is tight under our assumptions. Our results are exemplified through a case study on stochastic safe control.

</details>


### [108] [On the critical time of observability of the multi-dimensional Baouendi-Grushin equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01419)
*Jérémi Dardé,Mathilda Trabut*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究Baouendi-Grushin方程在张量化域上的可观测性，精确计算了最小可观测时间T*，并建立了T>T*时的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Baouendi-Grushin方程在张量化域上的可观测性性质，特别是确定最小可观测时间T*，这对于偏微分方程控制理论具有重要意义。

Method: 结合Carleman估计在环域或整个边界上的精细可观测性不等式，以及Lebeau-Robbiano策略来定位观测集。

Result: 精确计算了张量化观测集（内部观测ω×Ω̃和边界观测Γ×Ω̃）的最小可观测时间T*，并建立了T>T*时的充分条件。

Conclusion: 成功确定了Baouendi-Grushin方程在张量化域上的最小可观测时间，并通过Carleman估计和Lebeau-Robbiano策略的结合，为T>T*时的系统可观测性提供了充分条件。

Abstract: We investigate the observability properties of the Baouendi-Grushin equation on a tensorized domain $Ω:= \mathcal{B}_R \times \tilde Ω$, where $\mathcal{B}_R$ is the open ball of radius $R$ in dimension $d \ge 2$, and $\tilde Ω$ is a smooth, bounded, open set of arbitrary dimension. Our main result is a precise calculation of the minimal observability time $T^*$, for tensorized observation sets of the form $ω\times \tilde Ω$, with $ω\subset \mathcal{B}_R$ (internal observation), and $Γ\times \tilde Ω$, with $Γ\subset \partial \mathcal{B}_R$ (boundary observation).
  The main novelty regards the sufficient condition, that is observability of the system when $T>T^*$. This is established by combining refined observability inequalities on the annulus--or the entire boundary--using Carleman estimates, together with a Lebeau-Robbiano strategy to localize the observation sets.

</details>


### [109] [Rendezvous Planning from Sparse Observations of Optimally Controlled Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01428)
*Thomas A. Scott,Lukas Taus,Yen-Hsi Richard Tsai,Tan Bui-Thanh,Justin G. R. Delva*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出概率框架解决会合规划问题：基于稀疏噪声观测估计快速移动目标轨迹，为较慢的追踪智能体规划最优时空会合点，通过贪心最小化失败概率并更新信念空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自主会合应用中的不确定性规划问题，如无人机加油、航天器服务、自主水面舰艇操作、搜救任务和导弹防御等场景，其中追踪智能体速度显著慢于目标且观测稀疏噪声。

Method: 结合核基最大后验估计与高斯过程校正的滤波方法估计目标轨迹，生成轨迹假设混合分布；贪心选择时空会合点最小化当前信念空间中的失败概率；每次尝试后通过条件化更新信念空间，确保后续决策基于统计一致的剩余搜索空间表示。

Result: 推导了包含轨迹模型固有时间相关性的贝叶斯更新公式，证明了失败条件更新能正确捕获后续决策的后验信念，为不确定性下的规划提供了系统框架。

Conclusion: 该概率框架为自主会合应用中的不确定性规划提供了系统方法，通过贪心最小化失败概率和统计一致的信念更新，有效解决了稀疏噪声观测下快速移动目标的会合规划问题。

Abstract: We develop a probabilistic framework for \emph{rendezvous planning}: given sparse, noisy observations of a fast-moving target, plan rendezvous spatiotemporal coordinates for a set of significantly slower seeking agents. The unknown target trajectory is estimated under uncertain dynamics using a filtering approach that combines a kernel-based maximum a posteriori estimation with Gaussian process correction, producing a mixture over trajectory hypotheses. This estimate is used to select spatiotemporal rendezvous points that maximize the probability of successful rendezvous. Points are chosen sequentially by greedily minimizing failure probability in the current belief space, which is updated after each step by conditioning on unsuccessful rendezvous attempts. We show that the failure-conditioned update correctly captures the posterior belief for subsequent decisions, ensuring that each step in the greedy sequence is informed by a statistically consistent representation of the remaining search space, and derive the corresponding Bayesian updates incorporating temporal correlations intrinsic to the trajectory model. This result provides a systematic framework for planning under uncertainty in applications of autonomous rendezvous such as unmanned aerial vehicle refueling, spacecraft servicing, autonomous surface vessel operations, search and rescue missions, and missile defense. In each, the motion of the target entity can be modeled using a system of differential equations undergoing optimal control for a chosen objective, in our example case Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman solutions for minimum arrival time of a Dubins car with uncertain turning radius and destination.

</details>


### [110] [Discrete-Time Event-Triggered Extremum Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01450)
*Victor Hugo Pereira Rodrigues,Tiago Roux Oliveira,Miroslav Krstić,Frank Allgöwer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出离散时间事件触发极值搜索控制方案，用于非线性系统实时优化，通过状态依赖触发条件减少不必要执行和通信


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散时间极值搜索控制依赖周期性更新，导致不必要的执行器动作和通信开销。需要开发资源感知的实时优化方法，在保持优化能力的同时减少输入更新次数

Method: 结合极值搜索与事件触发机制，仅当状态依赖触发条件满足时才更新控制输入。使用离散时间平均法和Lyapunov分析，证明轨迹实际收敛到未知极值点邻域，并展示平均动态的指数稳定性

Result: 提出的方法在保持经典极值搜索优化能力的同时，显著减少了输入更新次数。仿真结果验证了该方法在资源感知实时优化中的有效性

Conclusion: 离散时间事件触发极值搜索控制方案成功实现了非线性系统实时优化，通过自适应确定输入更新时刻，在保证收敛性的同时减少了执行和通信开销

Abstract: This paper proposes a discrete-time event-triggered extremum seeking control scheme for real-time optimization of nonlinear systems. Unlike conventional discrete-time implementations relying on periodic updates, the proposed approach updates the control input only when a state-dependent triggering condition is satisfied, reducing unnecessary actuation and communication. The resulting closed-loop system combines extremum seeking with an event-triggering mechanism that adaptively determines the input update instants. Using discrete-time averaging and Lyapunov analysis, we establish practical convergence of the trajectories to a neighborhood of the unknown extremum point and show exponential stability of the associated average dynamics. The proposed method preserves the optimization capability of classical extremum seeking while significantly reducing the number of input updates. Simulation results illustrate the effectiveness of the approach for resource-aware real-time optimization.

</details>


### [111] [On Integral Linear Constraints on Convex Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01511)
*Emil Vladu,Alexandre Megretski,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究积分线性约束和具有线性供给率的耗散不等式，适用于点态约束在凸锥内的轨迹，建立了与有界线性泛函的等价关系，并应用于线性二次控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在凸锥约束下的积分线性约束和耗散不等式，建立与有界线性泛函的等价关系，为线性二次控制提供理论框架。

Method: 考虑有限维赋范向量空间中点态约束于凸锥的轨迹，证明积分线性约束和线性供给率耗散不等式当且仅当存在满足锥不等式有界线性泛函，类比二次供给率与线性矩阵不等式关系。

Result: 建立了凸锥约束下积分线性约束与有界线性泛函的等价关系，通过适当选择锥结构，应用于正系统理论中L1增益有界实引理类比，以及线性二次控制中非严格Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov引理。

Conclusion: 为凸锥约束轨迹的积分线性约束和耗散不等式提供了统一框架，建立了与有界线性泛函的等价关系，成功应用于线性二次控制理论，扩展了相关引理的应用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider integral linear constraints and the dissipation inequality with linear supply rates for certain sets of trajectories confined pointwise in time to a convex cone which belongs to a finite-dimensional normed vector space. Such constraints are then shown to be satisfied if and only if a bounded linear functional exists which satisfies a conic inequality. This is analogous to the typical situation in which a quadratic supply rate over the entire space is related to a linear matrix inequality. A connection is subsequently drawn precisely to linear-quadratic control: by proper choice of cone, the main results can be applied to produce a known L1-gain analogue to the bounded real lemma in positive systems theory, as well as a non-strict version of the Kalman-Yakubovich-Popov Lemma in linear-quadratic control.

</details>


### [112] [Strong Convergence of FISTA for Affinely Constrained Convex Quadratic Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01636)
*Sedi Bartz,Heinz H. Bauschke,Yuan Gao,Walaa M. Moursi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 证明了仿射二次凸优化问题中FISTA算法的强收敛性，无需闭性假设；特别地，对于无约束凸二次目标函数，Nesterov加速梯度法具有强收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决FISTA算法强收敛性的开放问题：2025年10月的研究证明了FISTA的弱收敛性，11月的研究在满足闭性条件下获得了仿射约束凸优化的强收敛结果。本文旨在消除闭性假设，在仿射二次情况下证明强收敛性。

Method: 专注于仿射二次凸优化问题，通过理论分析证明FISTA算法在该特定情况下的强收敛性，无需闭性假设。将结果特化到无约束凸二次目标函数，得到Nesterov加速梯度法的强收敛性。

Result: 成功证明了仿射二次凸优化问题中FISTA算法的强收敛性，无需任何闭性假设。特别地，对于无约束凸二次目标函数，Nesterov加速梯度法具有强收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文解决了FISTA算法强收敛性的重要开放问题，在仿射二次情况下无需闭性假设即可证明强收敛性，为凸优化算法的收敛性理论提供了重要进展。

Abstract: In October 2025, research by Boţ, Fadili, and Nguyen, and by Jang and Ryu, led to the seminal result that Beck and Teboulle's FISTA converges weakly to a minimizer of the sum of two convex functions resolving a long-standing open problem. The first strong convergence result was obtained in November 2025 by Moursi, Naguib, Pavlovic, and Vavasis for affinely constrained convex minimization provided certain closedness conditions hold.
  In this paper, we prove strong convergence in the affine-quadratic case without any closedness assumption. Specializing this to the unconstrained case, we obtain the strong convergence of Nesterov's accelerated gradient method when applied to a convex quadratic objective function.

</details>


### [113] [Day-Ahead Offering for Virtual Power Plants: A Stochastic Linear Programming Reformulation and Projected Subgradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01755)
*Weiqi Meng,Hongyi Li,Bai Cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于内近似投影次梯度方法解决虚拟电厂日前报价问题的两阶段随机自适应鲁棒优化框架，相比传统CC&G算法获得约两个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟电厂在日前市场中面临分布式能源输出和市场价格的间歇性与不确定性挑战，需要平衡利润机会与运行风险，传统方法计算效率不足。

Method: 1) 建立基于场景的两阶段随机自适应鲁棒优化模型，其中节点边际价格不确定性遵循马尔可夫过程，DER不确定性由静态不确定集描述；2) 基于列约束生成算法外近似原理，提出内近似投影次梯度方法；3) 在温和建模假设下，将鲁棒第二阶段问题等价重构为具有嵌套资源分配结构的线性规划，可采用高效贪心算法求解；4) 利用重构后第二阶段问题的计算效率和第一阶段可行域的单调结构，提出高效投影次梯度算法求解整体随机线性规划问题。

Result: 基于真实数据的广泛计算实验表明，整体投影次梯度下降方法相比CC&G算法在保持解质量的同时实现了约两个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: 提出的内近似投影次梯度方法为虚拟电厂日前报价问题提供了高效的计算框架，显著提升了求解效率，有助于虚拟电厂在实际运行中更好地应对不确定性和实现经济优化。

Abstract: Virtual power plants (VPPs) are an emerging paradigm that aggregates distributed energy resources (DERs) for coordinated participation in power systems, including bidding as a single dispatchable entity in the wholesale market. In this paper, we address a critical operational challenge for VPPs: the day-ahead offering problem under highly intermittent and uncertain DER outputs and market prices. The day-ahead offering problem determines the price-quantity pairs submitted by VPPs while balancing profit opportunities against operational uncertainties. First, we formulate the problem as a scenario-based two-stage stochastic adaptive robust optimization problem, where the uncertainty of the locational marginal prices follows a Markov process and DER uncertainty is characterized by static uncertainty sets. Then, motivated by the outer approximation principle of the column-and-constraint generation (CC&G) algorithm, we propose a novel inner approximation-based projected subgradient method. By exploiting the problem structure, we propose two novel approaches to improve computational tractability. First, we show that under mild modeling assumptions, the robust second-stage problem can be equivalently reformulated as a linear program (LP) with a nested resource allocation structure that is amenable to an efficient greedy algorithm. Furthermore, motivated by the computational efficiency of solving the reformulated primal second-stage problem and the isotonic structure of the first-stage feasible region, we propose an efficient projected subgradient algorithm to solve the overall stochastic LP problem. Extensive computational experiments using real-world data demonstrate that the overall projected subgradient descent method achieves about two orders of magnitude speedup over CC&G while maintaining solution quality.

</details>


### [114] [State-constrained optimal control of the continuity equation and infinite-dimensional viscosity solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01768)
*Fabio Bagagiolo,Ivan Romanò*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究带加权积分状态约束的连续性方程有限时域最优控制问题，在希尔伯特框架下分析密度函数，证明值函数的Lipschitz连续性及其满足的无穷维约束HJB方程（粘性解意义）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究连续性方程在固定集外质量加权积分约束下的最优控制问题，这类约束在人口动力学、交通流等应用中常见，需要发展无穷维约束HJB理论

Method: 在密度函数的希尔伯特框架下建立模型，在合适的紧不变子集上分析，采用动态规划方法，证明值函数满足约束HJB方程的粘性解性质（内部为下解，边界为上解）

Result: 证明了值函数的Lipschitz连续性，建立了约束HJB方程的粘性解理论，证明了比较原理和在Lipschitz类中的唯一性

Conclusion: 成功建立了带加权积分状态约束的连续性方程最优控制问题的无穷维HJB理论框架，为相关应用提供了理论基础

Abstract: We study a finite horizon optimal control problem for the continuity equation under a weighted integral state constraint on the mass outside a fixed set. The model is cast in a Hilbert framework for densities. On a suitable invariant compact subset, we prove that the value function is Lipschitz continuous and satisfies, by dynamic programming, the associated infinite dimensional constrained Hamilton Jacobi Bellman equation in viscosity sense (subsolution in the interior, supersolution up to the boundary). We finally prove a comparison principle and uniqueness in the Lipschitz class.

</details>


### [115] [Improving Operational Feasibility in Large-Scale Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01771)
*Gereon Recht,Oussama Alaya,Benedikt Jahn,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种通过重调度和增强初始解来解决大规模交流容量扩展问题的方法，确保交流可行性并支持后续动态电网仿真


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模电力系统规划主要使用线性化、仅考虑有功功率的潮流方程近似，忽略了许多运行约束，且仅在强简化假设下测试系统运行可行性。需要一种能获得交流容量扩展问题可行解的方法，确保稳态电压稳定性并支持后续动态仿真

Method: 通过重调度和增强初始解的方法获得交流容量扩展问题的解。问题考虑发电机、无功补偿设备、储能系统和输电的同时扩展，包括启动程序和功率源能力曲线的运行约束，以及电压角差和电压幅值的简化稳定性限制。测试多种现有和部分改进的潮流近似方法，将其集成到迭代输电扩展规划方法中，获得凸规划公式

Result: 在大不列颠和爱尔兰岛屿的大规模问题实例上验证方法，扩展开放数据源以建模无功功率。发现包含输电损耗来确定初始解最为关键，可减少获得交流可行解所需的重调度和增强量，而加入无功功率约束未带来进一步改进

Conclusion: 该方法在弱假设下确保交流可行系统，保证稳态电压稳定性并允许后续动态电网仿真，对于规划未来以逆变器为主的稳定电力系统至关重要

Abstract: Large-scale power system planning mostly uses linearized, active power only approximations of the power flow equations, ignores many operational constraints, and tests the operational feasibility of the resulting systems only under strongly simplifying assumptions. We propose an approach to obtain solutions to large instances of the alternating current capacity expansion problem via redispatch and reinforcement of an initial solution. The problem formulation considers simultaneous expansion of generators, reactive compensation devices, storage systems, and transmission. Furthermore, it includes operational constraints via startup procedures and capability curves of power sources and simplified stability limits via constraints on voltage angle differences and voltage magnitudes. To obtain initial solutions, we test several established and partly modified power flow approximations and integrate them into an approach for iterative transmission expansion planning, thereby obtaining convex formulations. We demonstrate the approach on large problem instances covering the islands of Great Britain and Ireland at the transmission level, for which we extend the open data source to model reactive power. We find that including transmission losses to determine the initial solution is most decisive, as the amount of redispatch and reinforcements necessary to obtain an alternating current feasible solution is reduced, whereas incorporating reactive power constraints did not lead to further improvements. Our approach ensures an alternating current feasible system under weak assumptions, thus guaranteeing steady-state voltage stability and allowing subsequent dynamic grid simulations, which is instrumental for planning stable future inverter-dominated power systems.

</details>


### [116] [Stability of the Timoshenko Beam Equation with One Weakly Degenerate Local Kelvin-Voigt Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01809)
*Ruijuan Liu,Qiong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有局部分布Kelvin-Voigt阻尼的Timoshenko梁方程，阻尼系数存在奇异性导致导数不连续，证明了系统具有多项式稳定性，衰减率为t^{-1/2}


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有奇异系数的局部Kelvin-Voigt阻尼对Timoshenko梁系统稳定性的影响，特别是当阻尼系数存在奇异性导致导数不连续时，系统的衰减行为

Method: 使用频域方法和乘子技术，分析阻尼作用于剪切应力或弯曲力矩两种情况，证明相关半群的多项式稳定性

Result: 无论局部Kelvin-Voigt阻尼作用于剪切应力还是弯曲力矩，系统都具有多项式稳定性，衰减率为t^{-1/2}

Conclusion: 具有奇异系数的局部Kelvin-Voigt阻尼Timoshenko梁系统表现出多项式衰减特性，衰减率与阻尼作用位置无关，均为t^{-1/2}

Abstract: We consider the Timoshenko beam equation with locally distributed Kelvin-Voigt damping, which affects either the shear stress or the bending moment. The damping coefficient exhibits a singularity, causing its derivative to be discontinuous. By using the frequency domain method and multiplier technique, we prove that the associated semigroup is polynomial stability. Specifically, regardless of whether the local Kelvin-Voigt damping acts on the shear stress or the bending moment, the system decays polynomially with rate $t^{-\frac{1}{2}}$.

</details>


### [117] [Hausdorff compactness and regularity for classes of open sets under geometric constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01813)
*Mohamed Barkatou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在ℝ²和ℝ³中引入具有内法向量几何性质的新型开集类，证明其在Hausdorff拓扑下的紧致性，建立Hausdorff、紧致和特征函数收敛的等价性，并研究厚度函数的正则性以及固定凸集C对可容许形状边界正则性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有特定几何性质的开集类，这些性质与内法向量相关，旨在建立这些集合类在Hausdorff拓扑下的紧致性结果，为形状优化和几何分析提供理论基础。

Method: 引入基于内法向量几何性质的新型开集类，使用Hausdorff拓扑分析集合收敛性，证明紧致性结果，建立不同收敛概念（Hausdorff、紧致、特征函数）的等价性，并分析厚度函数的正则性及其与固定凸集C的关系。

Result: 证明了所引入开集类在Hausdorff拓扑下的紧致性，建立了Hausdorff收敛、紧致收敛和特征函数收敛在某些类中的等价性，分析了厚度函数的正则性，并揭示了固定凸集C的正则性如何影响可容许形状的边界正则性。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了具有内法向量几何性质的开集类理论框架，建立了紧致性结果和收敛等价性，为形状优化和几何分析提供了重要的数学工具，同时揭示了凸集正则性与边界正则性之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: This article introduces innovative classes of open sets in \(\mathbb{R}^{N}\), where \(N=2, 3\), characterized by a geometric property associated with the inward normal. The focus lies on proving compactness results for the Hausdorff topology within these classes. Furthermore, the paper establishes the equivalence of convergences, encompassing Hausdorff, compact, and characteristic functions, for select classes. We also investigate the regularity of the thickness function associated with these domains and analyze how the regularity of the fixed convex set \(C\) influences the boundary regularity of the admissible shapes.

</details>


### [118] [Bilevel Programming Approach for Image Restoration Problems with Automatically Hyperparameter Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01866)
*Hang Xie,Xuewen Li,Peili Li,Qiuyu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双层规划的图像复原超参数自动选择方法，通过可行性惩罚函数和邻近点项解决非光滑凸规划问题，实现高质量复原并减少计算时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的图像复原模型中，超参数的正确选择对性能至关重要，但当前研究通常需要手动调参，耗时且不准确。需要开发自动选择超参数的方法以提高复原质量和效率。

Method: 采用双层规划方法同时选择最优超参数和实现高质量复原。将问题重新表述为包含凸差函数不等式约束的双层规划问题，通过可行性惩罚函数和邻近点项解决一系列非光滑凸规划问题。下层问题使用交替方向乘子法求解。

Result: 理论上证明算法生成的序列收敛到不等式约束等价双层规划问题的KKT稳定点。在仿真和真实图像上的测试表明，相比其他超参数选择方法，所提算法获得更优的复原质量且需要更少的计算时间。

Conclusion: 提出的双层规划方法能够有效自动选择图像复原中的超参数，在保证高质量复原的同时显著减少计算时间，为图像复原中的超参数优化问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In optimization-based image restoration models, the correct selection of hyperparameters is crucial for achieving superior performance. However, current research typically involves manual tuning of these hyperparameters, which is highly time-consuming and often lacks accuracy. In this paper, we concentrate on the automated selection of hyperparameters in the context of image restoration and present a bilevel programming approach that can simultaneously select the optimal hyperparameters and achieve high-quality restoration results. For implementation, we reformulate the bilevel programming problem that incorporates an inequality constraint related to the difference-of-convex functions. Following this, we address a sequence of nonsmooth convex programming problems by employing a feasibility penalty function along with a proximal point term. In this context, the nonsmooth convex programming problem uses the solution of the lower-level problem, which is derived through the alternating direction method of multipliers. Theoretically, we prove that the sequence generated by the algorithm converges to a Karush-Kuhn-Tucker stationary point of the inequality-constrained equivalent bilevel programming problem. We conduct a series of tests on both simulations and real images, which demonstrate that the proposed algorithm achieve superior restoration quality while requiring less computing time compared to other hyperparameter selection methods.

</details>


### [119] [Scaled Relative Graphs and Dynamic Integral Quadratic Constraints: Connections and Computations for Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01873)
*Timo de Groot,Tom Oomen,W. P. M. H. Heemels,Sebastiaan van den Eijnden*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于动态积分二次约束的系统方法，用于计算非线性系统的软硬SRG，并通过S-过程获得可处理的SRG上界，为Lur'e型系统提供新的图形化稳定性分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尺度相对图（SRG）为非线性系统提供了图形化分析和设计工具，但需要系统化的计算方法。本文旨在开发一种基于动态积分二次约束（IQC）的框架，以统一方式计算软硬SRG，并建立与经典IQC稳定性结果的图形化联系。

Method: 采用动态积分二次约束（IQC）方法，通过S-过程将IQC乘子转化为复平面区域，实现SRG的可计算上界。软SRG通过频域条件计算，硬SRG则通过乘子的硬分解和线性矩阵不等式获得。

Result: 建立了基于IQC的SRG计算方法，获得了软硬SRG的可处理上界。推导出Lur'e型系统的SRG反馈稳定性结果，为经典动态乘子IQC稳定性定理提供了新的图形化解释。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于动态IQC的SRG计算方法为非线性系统分析提供了系统化的图形化工具，将经典IQC稳定性理论与SRG框架统一起来，增强了非线性系统图形化分析和设计的能力。

Abstract: Scaled relative graphs (SRGs) enable graphical analysis and design of nonlinear systems. In this paper, we present a systematic approach for computing both soft and hard SRGs of nonlinear systems using dynamic integral quadratic constraints (IQCs). These constraints are exploited via application of the S-procedure to compute tractable SRG overbounds.
