In particular, we show that the multipliers associated with the IQCs define regions in the complex plane. Soft SRG computations are formulated through frequency-domain conditions, while hard SRGs are obtained via hard factorizations of multipliers and linear matrix inequalities. The overbounds are used to derive an SRG-based feedback stability result for Lur'e-type systems, providing a new graphical interpretation of classical IQC stability results with dynamic multipliers.

</details>


### [120] [When does learning pay off? A study on DRL-based dynamic algorithm configuration for carbon-aware scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01886)
*Andrea Mencaroni,Robbert Reijnen,Yingqian Zhang,Dieter Claeys*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: DRL-based动态算法配置框架在碳感知置换流水车间调度问题中表现出良好的泛化能力，训练于小型简单实例后能在更复杂未见实例上持续超越静态调优基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究深度强化学习在动态算法配置中的实际泛化能力，验证训练成本是否值得投资，特别是在真实世界优化问题中，当问题规模和实例特性变化时，学习到的控制策略能否有效迁移

Method: 开发基于深度强化学习的动态算法配置框架，仅在小规模简单实例上进行训练，然后将学习到的策略部署到相似和更复杂的未见实例上，并与静态调优基线进行公平比较

Result: 在训练和调优阶段观察到的简单实例上，DRL表现与静态调优基线相当；但随着实例特性差异增大和计算复杂度增加，DRL学习到的策略持续超越静态调优；证明DRL能够获得鲁棒且可泛化的控制策略

Conclusion: DRL能够学习到超越训练实例分布的鲁棒泛化控制策略，这种跨实例类型的泛化能力使得初始计算投资具有价值，特别是在静态调优难以适应变化问题场景的环境中

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently emerged as a promising tool for Dynamic Algorithm Configuration (DAC), enabling evolutionary algorithms to adapt their parameters online rather than relying on static tuned configurations. While DRL can learn effective control policies, training is computationally expensive. This cost may be justified if learned policies generalize, allowing the training effort to transfer across instance types and problem scales. Yet, for real-world optimization problems, it remains unclear whether this promise holds in practice and under which conditions the investment in learning pays off. In this work, we investigate this question in the context of the carbon-aware permutation flow-shop scheduling problem. We develop a DRL-based DAC framework and train it exclusively on small, simple instances. We then deploy the learned policy on both similar and more complex unseen instances and compare its performance against a static tuned baseline, which provides a fair point of comparison. Our findings show that the proposed method provides a strong dynamic algorithm control policy that can be effectively transferred to different unseen problem instances. Notably, on simple and cheap to compute instances, similar to those observed during training and tuning, DRL performs comparably with the statically tuned baseline. However, as instance characteristics diverge and computational complexities increase, the DRL-learned policy continuously outperforms static tuning. These results confirm that DRL can acquire robust and generalizable control policies which are effective beyond the training instance distributions. This ability to generalize across instance types makes the initial computational investment worthwhile, particularly in settings where static tuning struggles to adapt to changing problem scenarios.

</details>


### [121] [Output Corridor Impulsive Control of First-order Continuous System with Non-local Attractivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01959)
*Alexander Medvedev,Anton V. Proskurnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 设计用于连续标量时不变线性系统的脉冲控制器，适用于化学动力学模型，特别适合工程和医学中的离散给药应用，通过构建稳定的1周期解将输出限制在预定范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对化学动力学中普遍存在的连续标量时不变线性系统，解决离散给药应用中剂量和给药间隔的联合控制问题，这类系统在过程控制和药代动力学中具有重要应用价值。

Method: 设计脉冲反馈控制器，通过振幅和频率调制在离散时间点作用；构建稳定的1周期解（1-cycle），使其一维轨道与预定输出范围重合；建立1周期解的局部和全局吸引性条件。

Result: 提出了将系统输出限制在预定范围内的脉冲控制方法，建立了1周期解的稳定性和吸引性条件，并以静脉注射对乙酰氨基酚的给药问题作为数值示例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该脉冲控制方法适用于具有Wiener结构的连续线性系统，特别适合离散给药应用，能够通过设计稳定的1周期解实现输出范围的精确控制，为工程和医学中的剂量控制问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the design of an impulsive controller for a continuous scalar time-invariant linear plant that constitutes the simplest conceivable model of chemical kinetics. The model is ubiquitous in process control as well as pharmacometrics and readily generalizes to systems of Wiener structure. Given the impulsive nature of the feedback, the control problem formulation is particularly suited to discrete dosing applications in engineering and medicine, where both doses and inter-dose intervals are manipulated. Since the feedback controller acts at discrete time instants and employs both amplitude and frequency modulation, whereas the plant is continuous, the closed-loop system exhibits hybrid dynamics featuring complex nonlinear phenomena. The problem of confining the plant output to a predefined corridor of values is considered. The method at the heart of the proposed approach is to design a stable periodic solution, called a 1-cycle, whose one-dimensional orbit coincides with the predefined corridor. Conditions ensuring local and global attractivity of the 1-cycle are established. As a numerical illustration of the proposed approach, the problem of intravenous paracetamol dosing is considered.

</details>


### [122] [Balancing Morality and Economics: Population Games with Herding and Inertia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02030)
*Raghupati Vyas,Harsitha Devaraj,Veeraruna Kavitha*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究消费者异质行为倾向下的清洁技术采用，建立多类型平均场博弈模型，分析理性、从众和惰性三类消费者的技术选择，发现人口构成对清洁技术采用的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 清洁技术采用对减少二氧化碳排放至关重要，但现实中消费者具有异质行为倾向，需要研究不同类型消费者在技术选择中的相互作用及其对整体采用水平的影响。

Method: 建立多类型平均场博弈模型，将消费者分为理性（权衡价格和道德激励）、从众（跟随多数）和惰性（对新技术有惯性）三类。使用α-理性纳什均衡及其多类型扩展来表征均衡采用水平，并通过随机轮换行为动力学的极限识别稳定子集。

Result: 人口构成对清洁技术采用起决定性作用：广泛采用需要清洁技术价格劣势足够小，或者存在足够大的从众人群可通过社会意识项目影响。令人惊讶的是，环境损害本身不足以激励清洁技术采用增加。

Conclusion: 清洁技术推广策略应针对不同行为类型消费者设计：对理性消费者需降低价格劣势，对从众人群可通过社会影响项目推动，而环境损害本身不是有效激励因素。

Abstract: The adoption of clean technologies (CTs) plays an important role in reducing carbon dioxide (CO$_2$) emissions. We study CT adoption in a large population of consumers with heterogeneous behavioral tendencies. We model the interaction among the agents as a multi-type mean-field game in which the agents choose between clean and polluting technology based products and may either behave as rationals (trading off price and moral incentives), herding agents (just follow the majority), or lethargic agents exhibiting inertia toward adopting the new technologies. We characterize equilibrium CT adoption levels using the recently introduced notion of $\boldsymbolα$-Rational Nash Equilibrium ($\boldsymbolα$-RNE) and its multi-type extension. We then identify a stable subset using the limits of a stochastic turn-by-turn behavioral dynamics. Our results highlight the role of population composition in determining CT adoption. In particular, widespread adoption requires either a sufficiently small price disadvantage for CTs or the presence of a sufficiently large herding population that can be influenced through social awareness programs. Surprisingly, we could prove that environmental damages do not provide sufficient incentives to increase CT adoption.

</details>


### [123] [Faster Symmetric Rendezvous on Four or More Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02058)
*Javier Cembrano,Felix Fischer,Max Klimm*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明Anderson-Weber策略对于n≥4的对称约会问题不是最优的，通过构造具有更小期望相遇时间的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称约会问题中Anderson-Weber策略的最优性，该策略已知在n=2和n=3时最优，但需要验证对于更大n值是否仍然最优。

Method: 构造了一个新的策略，该策略具有比Anderson-Weber策略更小的期望相遇时间，从而证明原策略对于n≥4不是最优的。

Result: 成功证明Anderson-Weber策略对于所有n≥4都不是最优的，通过构造的策略实现了更小的期望相遇时间。

Conclusion: Anderson-Weber策略仅对n=2和n=3是最优的，对于n≥4存在更好的策略，这解决了对称约会问题中该策略最优性范围的长期疑问。

Abstract: In the symmetric rendezvous problem two players follow the same (randomized) strategy to visit one of $n$ locations in each time step $t=0,1,2,\dots$. Their goal is to minimize the expected time until they visit the same location and thus meet. Anderson and Weber [J. Appl. Prob., 1990] proposed a strategy that operates in rounds of $n-1$ steps: a player either remains in one location for $n-1$ steps or visits the other $n-1$ locations in random order; the choice between these two options is made with a probability that depends only on $n$. The strategy is known to be optimal for $n=2$ and $n=3$, and there is convincing evidence that it is not optimal for $n=4$. We show that it is not optimal for any $n\geq 4$, by constructing a strategy with a smaller expected meeting time.

</details>


### [124] [Fixed-time-stable ODE Representation of Lasso](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02069)
*Liang Wu,Yunhong Che,Wallace Gian Yion Tan,Efstathios Iliakis,Richard D. Braatz,Ján Drgoňa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种固定时间稳定的ODE表示方法来解决Lasso问题，通过将其转化为光滑非负二次规划，并设计无投影的牛顿型固定时间稳定ODE系统来求解KKT条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lasso问题在信号处理、机器学习和控制等领域广泛存在，并与神经科学中观察到的稀疏编码机制密切相关。连续时间ODE表示不仅能在模拟计算机上求解Lasso问题，还为解释神经生理现象提供了框架。

Method: 首先将Lasso问题转化为光滑的非负二次规划问题，然后设计无投影的牛顿型固定时间稳定ODE系统来求解对应的KKT条件。该ODE的稳定时间与问题数据无关且可任意设定。

Result: 数值实验验证了轨迹在预设时间内达到最优解，证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种固定时间稳定的ODE表示方法，为Lasso问题提供了新的求解框架，具有稳定时间可预设且与数据无关的优点，在模拟计算和神经科学解释方面具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Lasso problems arise in many areas, including signal processing, machine learning, and control, and are closely connected to sparse coding mechanisms observed in neuroscience. A continuous-time ordinary differential equation (ODE) representation of the Lasso problem not only enables its solution on analog computers but also provides a framework for interpreting neurophysiological phenomena. This article proposes a fixed-time-stable ODE representation of the Lasso problem by first transforming it into a smooth nonnegative quadratic program (QP) and then designing a projection-free Newton-based ODE representation of the Lasso problem by first transforming it into a smooth nonnegative quadratic program (QP) and then designing a projection-free Newton-based fixed-time-stable ODE system for solving the corresponding Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. Moreover, the settling time of the ODE is independent of the problem data and can be arbitrarily prescribed. Numerical experiments verify that the trajectory reaches the optimal solution within the prescribed time.

</details>


### [125] [Safe Control of Feedback-Interconnected Systems via Singular Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02132)
*Stefano Di Gregorio,Guido Carnevale,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于奇异摄动理论的方法，将简化模型的安全证书扩展到具有不同时间尺度的反馈互联系统，通过设计复合控制障碍函数来保证整个系统的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代复杂系统通常包含不同时间尺度的反馈互联子系统（如机器人系统中的电气和机械部分），安全约束往往只涉及系统的一部分。现有方法难以直接处理这种多时间尺度系统的安全认证问题。

Method: 受奇异摄动稳定性分析的启发，开发了一种形式化程序，将简化模型设计的安全证书提升到整个反馈互联系统。在慢动态和快动态具有足够时间尺度分离的条件下，设计复合控制障碍函数来认证互联系统安全集的前向不变性。

Result: 理论证明在足够时间尺度分离条件下，复合CBF能够保证互联系统的安全集前向不变性。在线安全滤波器只需在低维简化模型上求解。在机器人臂关节电机动力学和优化算法驱动的物理系统上进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: 该方法为具有不同时间尺度反馈互联的复杂系统提供了一种有效的安全认证框架，显著降低了在线安全滤波的计算复杂度，同时保持了整个系统的安全性保证。

Abstract: Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) have emerged as a powerful tool in the design of safety-critical controllers for nonlinear systems. In modern applications, complex systems often involve the feedback interconnection of subsystems evolving at different timescales, e.g., two parts from different physical domains (e.g., the electrical and mechanical parts of robotic systems) or a physical plant and an (optimization or control) algorithm. In these scenarios, safety constraints often involve only a portion of the overall system. Inspired by singular perturbations for stability analysis, we develop a formal procedure to lift a safety certificate designed on a reduced-order model to the overall feedback-interconnected system. Specifically, we show that under a sufficient timescale separation between slow and fast dynamics, a composite CBF can be designed to certify the forward invariance of the safe set for the interconnected system. As a result, the online safety filter only needs to be solved for the lower-dimensional, reduced-order model. We numerically test the proposed approach on: (i) a robotic arm with joint motor dynamics, and (ii) a physical plant driven by an optimization algorithm.

</details>


### [126] [Data-Driven Tube-Based Zonotopic Predictive Control With Nonconvex Layered Terminal Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02159)
*Zhen Zhang,Bogdan Gheorghe,Florin Stoican,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于数据驱动的管状zonotopic预测控制框架，采用非凸分层终端集设计，解决了传统单椭球终端集保守性限制可行性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于数据驱动的管状zonotopic预测控制方案通常依赖单个椭球终端集，这种方法较为保守，限制了控制器的可行性。需要一种能够解耦稳定性认证、可行性扩展和运动区域筛选的设计方法。

Method: 提出了分层终端集设计：1）离线设计反馈增益和收缩约束zonotope用于稳定性认证；2）推导闭环模型逆集的数据驱动表征，避免区间矩阵松弛和求逆的保守性；3）结合精确集合乘法，获得固定闭环动态下最大鲁棒正不变集的内外近似，内近似作为非凸终端集扩展可行性，外近似提供认证的运动区域描述用于快速筛选和监控。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法相比现有凸终端DTZPC方案，能够获得更紧的逆集包络和更好的可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的分层终端集设计有效解耦了稳定性认证、可行性扩展和运动区域筛选，通过非凸终端集设计显著提高了控制器的可行性，同时避免了高维DTZPC中的概率反馈合成。

Abstract: This paper presents a data-driven tube-based zonotopic predictive control (DTZPC) framework with nonconvex layered terminal sets. Existing DTZPC schemes with closed-loop guarantees typically rely on a single ellipsoidal terminal set, which can be conservative and thereby limit feasibility. We propose a layered terminal-set design that decouples stability certification, feasibility enlargement, and motion-region screening into three components with distinct roles. First, an offline-designed feedback gain together with a contractive constrained zonotope provides a terminal ingredient for stability certification, while avoiding probabilistic feedback synthesis in high-dimensional DTZPC. Second, we derive a data-driven characterization of the inverse admissible closed-loop model set, avoiding the conservatism of interval-matrix relaxation and inversion. Combined with exact set multiplication, this yields inner and outer approximations of the maximal robust positively invariant (MRPI) set under fixed closed-loop dynamics. The inner approximation serves as a nonconvex terminal set to enlarge feasibility, whereas the outer approximation provides certified motion-region descriptions for fast screening and monitoring. Numerical examples demonstrate tighter inverse-set enclosures and improved feasibility over existing convex-terminal DTZPC schemes.

</details>


### [127] [Explicit Distributed MPC: Reducing Computation and Communication Load by Exploiting Facet Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02177)
*Parth R. Brahmbhatt,Hari S. Ganesh,Styliani Avraamidou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出FACET-DiMPC方法，利用面特性进行针对性临界区域探索，显著降低计算复杂度，相比经典迭代DiMPC减少98%平均计算时间，相比无迭代DiMPC减少42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典分布式模型预测控制需要多次迭代才能收敛，导致计算和通信负担高。需要改进无迭代分布式MPC方法，减少计算量和通信负载。

Method: 基于多参数规划离线计算各子系统的显式控制律，实现实时控制而无需子系统间迭代数据交换。引入基于面特性的临界区域探索技术（FACET-DiMPC），利用面特性进行针对性临界区域探索，进一步降低计算复杂度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法达到与集中式方法相当的控制性能，同时显著减少通信开销和计算时间。相比经典迭代DiMPC方法平均计算时间减少98%，相比无迭代DiMPC方法减少42%。

Conclusion: FACET-DiMPC方法通过利用面特性进行针对性临界区域探索，显著提高了计算效率，适用于具有严格延迟和计算约束的实时控制应用。

Abstract: Classical Distributed Model Predictive Control (DiMPC) requires multiple iterations to achieve convergence, leading to high computational and communication burdens. This work focuses on the improvement of an iteration-free distributed MPC methodology that minimizes computational effort and communication load. The aforementioned methodology leverages multiparametric programming to compute explicit control laws offline for each subsystem, enabling real-time control without iterative data exchanges between subsystems. Extending our previous work on iteration-free DiMPC, here we introduce a FAcet-based Critical region Exploration Technique for iteration-free DiMPC (FACET-DiMPC) that further reduces computational complexity by leveraging facet properties to do targeted critical region exploration. Simulation results demonstrate that the developed method achieves comparable control performance to centralized methods, while significantly reducing communication overhead and computation time. In particular, the proposed methodology offers substantial efficiency gains in terms of the average computation time reduction of 98% compared to classic iterative DiMPC methods and 42% compared to iteration-free DiMPC methods, making it well-suited for real-time control applications with tight latency and computation constraints.

</details>


### [128] [Random-Subspace Sequential Quadratic Programming for Constrained Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02202)
*Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种零阶随机子空间序列二次规划方法(ZO-RS-SQP)，通过低维子空间采样和两点方向估计，在仅使用函数值的情况下高效求解高维非线性约束优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶方法在高维空间中依赖噪声梯度估计，难以同时实现计算效率和精确约束满足。需要开发一种既能降低计算成本又能保持SQP结构优势的方法。

Method: 提出ZO-RS-SQP方法：每轮迭代采样随机低维子空间，使用两点方向估计法估计投影后的目标梯度和约束雅可比矩阵，求解降维二次规划计算步长。还包含Armijo线搜索变体以提高鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准光滑性和正则性假设下，以高概率收敛到一阶KKT点。数值实验验证了该方法在非线性约束问题上的有效性。每次迭代的评估成本仅与子空间维度相关，而非环境维度。

Conclusion: ZO-RS-SQP成功结合了低维子空间采样与SQP结构，在仅使用函数值的情况下实现了高维约束优化的高效求解，平衡了计算效率和约束满足精度。

Abstract: We study nonlinear constrained optimization problems in which only function evaluations of the objective and constraints are available. Existing zeroth-order methods rely on noisy gradient and Jacobian surrogates in high dimensions, making it difficult to simultaneously achieve computational efficiency and accurate constraint satisfaction. We propose a zeroth-order random-subspace sequential quadratic programming method (ZO-RS-SQP) that combines two-point directional estimation with low-dimensional SQP updates. At each iteration, the method samples a random low-dimensional subspace, estimates the projected objective gradient and constraint Jacobians using two-point evaluations, and solves a reduced quadratic program to compute the step. As a result, the per-iteration evaluation cost scales with the subspace dimension rather than the ambient dimension, while retaining the structured linearized-constraint treatment of SQP. We also consider an Armijo line-search variant that improves robustness in practice. Under standard smoothness and regularity assumptions, we establish convergence to first-order KKT points with high probability. Numerical experiments illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach on nonlinear constrained problems.

</details>


### [129] [On the Convexity of the Solution Set of Linear Complementarity Problem over Tensor Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02231)
*Sonali Sharma,V. Vetrivel,Jein-Shan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究张量空间线性互补问题(TLCP)解集的凸性，引入T-列充分张量概念，建立解集凸性的等价条件，推导唯一性和可行性蕴含可解性的充分条件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量空间线性互补问题在优化、博弈论和工程中有广泛应用，但对其解集性质的研究相对有限。特别是解集的凸性条件尚未明确，这限制了相关算法的设计和理论分析。

Method: 引入T-列充分张量概念，研究其性质及其与多种结构张量的关系。建立TLCP解集凸性的等价条件，推导唯一性和可行性蕴含可解性的充分条件。

Result: 建立了TLCP解集凸性的等价条件，该条件与T-列充分张量密切相关。推导了TLCP解唯一性的充分条件，以及可行性蕴含可解性的充分条件。

Conclusion: T-列充分张量是理解TLCP解集凸性的关键概念。建立的等价条件为TLCP的理论分析和算法设计提供了重要基础，相关结果扩展了传统线性互补问题的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper investigates the convexity of the solution set of the linear complementarity problems over tensor spaces (TLCPs). We introduce the notion of a $T$-column sufficient tensor and study its properties and relationships with several structured tensors. An equivalent condition for the convexity of the solution set of the $\mathrm{TLCP}$ is established. In addition, sufficient conditions for uniqueness and for feasibility implying solvability are derived.

</details>


### [130] [Analytic Optimal Control for a Class of Driftless x-Flat Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02249)
*Raphael Buchinger,Georg Hartl,Lukas Ecker,Markus Schöberl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对三状态两输入的无漂移x-平坦非线性系统，提出了一种最优轨迹跟踪方法，通过利用几何特性和权重矩阵关系，推导出协态和输入反馈的闭式解，避免了数值求解两点边值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无漂移x-平坦非线性系统的最优轨迹跟踪问题，旨在避免传统方法中需要数值求解两点边值问题的计算复杂性，通过解析方法获得最优控制律。

Method: 采用Bolza形式表述跟踪问题，应用Pontryagin极大值原理得到混合正则-奇异最优控制问题。利用系统几何特性和权重矩阵的特定关系，推导出协态和输入反馈的闭式表达式。奇异输入导致bang-singular-bang最优控制结构，在奇异弧上跟踪误差动力学简化为二阶线性系统。

Result: 成功推导出协态和输入反馈的闭式表达式，避免了数值求解两点边值问题。在可转向轴运动学模型上验证了该方法，实现了精确的轨迹跟踪。

Conclusion: 针对特定类型的非线性系统，通过解析方法解决了最优轨迹跟踪问题，避免了数值求解的复杂性，为无漂移x-平坦系统提供了一种高效的最优控制设计框架。

Abstract: This paper studies optimal trajectory-tracking for driftless, x-flat nonlinear systems with three states and two inputs. The tracking problem is formulated in Bolza form with a quadratic cost of the tracking error and its derivative. Applying Pontryagin's maximum principle yields a mixed regular-singular optimal control problem. By exploiting geometric properties and a specific relation between the weighting matrices, a closed-form expression for the costate and an explicit feedback law for both inputs is derived. Thereby, the numerical solution of a two-point boundary-value problem is avoided. The singular input leads to a bang-singular-bang optimal control structure, while on the singular arc, the tracking error dynamics reduces to a linear dynamics of order two. The approach is illustrated for the kinematic model of a steerable axle, demonstrating accurate trajectory-tracking.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Runtime Burden Allocation for Structured LLM Routing in Agentic Expert Systems: A Full-Factorial Cross-Backend Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01235)
*Zhou Hanlin,Chan Huah Yong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化LLM路由本质上是一个系统层面的负载分配问题，而非简单的提示工程问题，并证明不同后端模型的最优路由策略存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型成为智能体AI系统的核心控制组件，可靠的结构化路由需要在正确性、延迟和实施成本之间取得平衡。当前研究往往将结构化路由视为提示工程问题，但作者认为这更根本地是一个系统层面的负载分配问题，涉及如何在生成堆栈中分配结构化工作。

Method: 通过全面的全因子基准测试，评估了48种部署配置和15,552个请求，覆盖OpenAI、Gemini和Llama三种后端模型。研究比较了三种结构化工作分配方式：模型直接输出结构、传输过程中压缩结构、生成后本地重建结构。

Result: 核心发现：不存在通用的最佳路由模式。后端特定的交互效应主导性能表现。在Gemini和OpenAI上保持高可靠性的模式，在Llama上可能出现显著的正确性下降；压缩实现的效率增益也强烈依赖于后端模型。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个可部署的框架，用于在异构后端条件下推理结构化路由问题。提供了跨后端评估方法和实际部署指导，帮助在生产级智能体专家系统中导航正确性-成本-延迟的权衡边界。

Abstract: Structured LLM routing is often treated as a prompt-engineering problem. We argue that it is, more fundamentally, a systems-level burden-allocation problem. As large language models (LLMs) become core control components in agentic AI systems, reliable structured routing must balance correctness, latency, and implementation cost under real deployment constraints. We show that this balance is shaped not only by prompts or schemas, but also by how structural work is allocated across the generation stack: whether output structure is emitted directly by the model, compressed during transport, or reconstructed locally after generation.
  We evaluate this formulation through a comprehensive full-factorial benchmark covering 48 deployment configurations and 15,552 requests across OpenAI, Gemini, and Llama backends. Our central finding is consequential: there is no universal best routing mode. Instead, backend-specific interaction effects dominate performance. Modes that remain highly reliable on Gemini and OpenAI can suffer substantial correctness degradation on Llama, while efficiency gains from compressed realization are strongly backend-dependent.
  Rather than presenting another isolated model comparison, this work contributes a deployable framework for reasoning about structured routing under heterogeneous backend conditions. We provide a cross-backend evaluation methodology and practical deployment guidance for navigating the correctness-cost-latency frontier in production-grade agentic expert systems.

</details>


### [132] [The Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework: A Validation Architecture for Simulated Potential Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01325)
*Olav Laudy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出数字孪生反事实框架，通过模拟而非统计估计反事实结果，建立分层验证体系，将不可验证的因果主张转化为可检验的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断方法依赖不可观测的假设（如可忽略性、平行趋势等）来填补缺失的反事实数据，但这些假设本身无法被验证。需要一种新框架将因果主张转化为可检验的形式。

Method: 提出数字孪生反事实框架：1）使用数字孪生模拟器在潜在结果框架内生成反事实结果；2）建立孪生保真度假设层次结构（边际保真度→联合保真度→结构保真度）；3）设计五层验证架构将不可验证的因果主张转化为可检验的假设；4）形式化分解将因果量区分为可边际验证和依赖copula的部分。

Result: 1）建立了将不可验证的因果主张转化为可检验假设的验证架构；2）形式化区分了可边际验证的因果量（ATE、CATE、QTE）和依赖copula的因果量（ITE分布、获益/损害概率、处理效应方差）；3）提供了边界、敏感性和不确定性量化工具来显式处理copula依赖。

Conclusion: 数字孪生反事实框架不解决因果推断的根本问题，但提供了：1）使边际因果主张变得可检验；2）使联合因果主张变得显式依赖假设；3）形式化表征两者之间的差距。该框架为因果推断提供了更透明、可验证的方法论基础。

Abstract: The fundamental problem of causal inference - that the counterfactual outcome for any individual is never observed - has shaped the entire methodology of the field. Every existing approach substitutes assumptions for missing data: ignorability, parallel trends, exclusion restrictions. None produces the counterfactual itself. This paper proposes the Digital Twin Counterfactual Framework (DTCF): rather than estimating the counterfactual statistically, we simulate it using a digital twin and subject the simulation to a hierarchical validation regime. We formalize the digital twin simulator as a stochastic mapping within the potential outcomes framework and introduce a hierarchy of twin fidelity assumptions - from marginal fidelity through joint fidelity to structural fidelity - each unlocking a progressively richer class of estimands. The central contribution is threefold. First, a five-level validation architecture converts the unfalsifiable claim that the simulator produces correct counterfactuals into falsifiable tests against observable data. Second, a formal decomposition separates causal quantities into those that are marginally validated (ATE, CATE, QTE - testable through observable-arm comparison) and those that are copula-dependent (the ITE distribution, probability of benefit/harm, variance of treatment effects - permanently reliant on the unobservable within-individual dependence structure). Third, bounding, sensitivity, and uncertainty quantification tools make the copula dependence explicit. The DTCF does not resolve the fundamental problem of causal inference. What it provides is a framework in which marginal causal claims become increasingly testable, joint causal claims become explicitly assumption-indexed, and the gap between the two is formally characterized.

</details>


### [133] [IDEA2: Expert-in-the-loop competency question elicitation for collaborative ontology engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01344)
*Elliott Watkiss-Leek,Reham Alharbi,Harry Rostron,Andrew Ng,Ewan Johnson,Andrew Mitchell,Terry R. Payne,Valentina Tamma,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IDEA2是一个结合大型语言模型和专家在环的半自动化工作流，用于从需求文档中提取和优化能力问题，以解决本体工程中资源密集的CQ获取瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能力问题获取是本体工程中的关键但资源密集型瓶颈，受到领域专家（拥有知识）和本体工程师（形式化知识）之间沟通鸿沟的阻碍，需要更高效、协作的方法来改进这一过程。

Method: IDEA2采用半自动化工作流，核心是迭代循环：1）LLM从需求文档中初步提取CQ；2）领域专家在协作平台上进行共创式评审和反馈；3）LLM根据反馈迭代重新表述被拒绝的CQ直至达成共识。使用溯源模型跟踪每个CQ的完整生命周期，包括编辑谱系、匿名反馈和生成参数。

Result: 在2个真实场景（科学数据、文化遗产）中验证表明，IDEA2能够加速需求工程过程，提高最终CQ的接受度和相关性，并在领域专家中展现出高可用性和有效性。

Conclusion: IDEA2通过集成LLM和专家在环协作，有效解决了本体工程中CQ获取的瓶颈问题，提高了过程的效率和结果质量，所有代码和实验均已开源。

Abstract: Competency question (CQ) elicitation represents a critical but resource-intensive bottleneck in ontology engineering. This foundational phase is often hampered by the communication gap between domain experts, who possess the necessary knowledge, and ontology engineers, who formalise it. This paper introduces IDEA2, a novel, semi-automated workflow that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) within a collaborative, expert-in-the-loop process to address this challenge. The methodology is characterised by a core iterative loop: an initial LLM-based extraction of CQs from requirement documents, a co-creational review and feedback phase by domain experts on an accessible collaborative platform, and an iterative, feedback-driven reformulation of rejected CQs by an LLM until consensus is achieved. To ensure transparency and reproducibility, the entire lifecycle of each CQ is tracked using a provenance model that captures the full lineage of edits, anonymised feedback, and generation parameters. The workflow was validated in 2 real-world scenarios (scientific data, cultural heritage), demonstrating that IDEA2 can accelerate the requirements engineering process, improve the acceptance and relevance of the resulting CQs, and exhibit high usability and effectiveness among domain experts. We release all code and experiments at https://github.com/KE-UniLiv/IDEA2

</details>


### [134] [Semantic Modeling for World-Centered Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01359)
*Andrei Mantsivoda,Darya Gavrilina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以世界为中心的多智能体系统（WMAS）作为传统智能体中心架构的替代方案，强调结构化领域需要共享的显式世界表示来确保语义一致性、可解释性和长期稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统智能体中心架构在结构化领域（如企业、制度系统）中存在语义不一致、可解释性差和长期稳定性不足的问题，需要一种共享的显式世界表示来确保全局一致性。

Method: 1. 提出世界中心多智能体系统（WMAS）架构；2. 沿本体显式性、规范性等维度对世界进行分类；3. 在共享世界模型上进行学习和协调；4. 提出语义模型作为形式化表示；5. 开发Ontobox平台作为WMAS的实现。

Result: 提出了完整的WMAS理论框架，包括世界分类体系、基于共享世界模型的学习协调机制、语义模型形式化方法，以及Ontobox平台作为具体实现。

Conclusion: WMAS通过共享的显式世界表示解决了结构化多智能体系统中的语义一致性和可解释性问题，为复杂系统提供了更稳定、可验证的架构方案。

Abstract: We introduce world-centered multi-agent systems (WMAS) as an alternative to traditional agent-centered architectures, arguing that structured domains such as enterprises and institutional systems require a shared, explicit world representation to ensure semantic consistency, explainability, and long-term stability. We classify worlds along dimensions including ontological explicitness, normativity, etc. In WMAS, learning and coordination operate over a shared world model rather than isolated agent-local representations, enabling global consistency and verifiable system behavior. We propose semantic models as a mathematical formalism for representing such worlds. Finally, we present the Ontobox platform as a realization of WMAS.

</details>


### [135] [Crashing Waves vs. Rising Tides: Preliminary Findings on AI Automation from Thousands of Worker Evaluations of Labor Market Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01363)
*Matthias Mertens,Adam Kuzee,Brittany S. Harris,Harry Lyu,Wensu Li,Jonathan Rosenfeld,Meiri Anto,Martin Fleming,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出AI自动化是一个连续体，包括"崩溃波"（AI能力在少量任务上突然激增）和"上升潮"（AI能力持续广泛提升）。基于对3000多个文本任务的评估，研究发现上升潮是主要的AI自动化形式，AI性能正在广泛快速提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在理解AI自动化的本质模式，区分"崩溃波"（AI能力突然激增）和"上升潮"（AI能力持续广泛提升）两种不同模式，以更准确地预测AI对经济和劳动力市场的影响。

Method: 使用美国劳工部O*NET分类中的3000多个文本任务进行评估，这些任务都是基于文本且LLM可处理的。通过来自这些工作岗位的工人进行了超过17,000次评估，测试AI在不同任务上的表现。

Result: 研究发现几乎没有"崩溃波"的证据（与METR近期研究相反），但发现了显著的"上升潮"证据。AI在广泛任务上表现良好且快速提升：2024年第二季度，AI模型能以约50%的成功率完成人类需要3-4小时的任务；到2025年第三季度，成功率将提升至约65%。如果当前趋势持续，到2029年LLM在大多数文本任务上的平均成功率将达到80%-95%。

Conclusion: AI自动化主要表现为"上升潮"模式，AI能力正在广泛持续提升而非突然激增。这种能力提升将对经济和组织产生重要影响，但组织采用AI的过程可能需要更长时间。研究强调了理解AI自动化模式对预测其社会经济影响的重要性。

Abstract: We propose that AI automation is a continuum between: (i) crashing waves where AI capabilities surge abruptly over small sets of tasks, and (ii) rising tides where the increase in AI capabilities is more continuous and broad-based. We test for these effects in preliminary evidence from an ongoing evaluation of AI capabilities across over 3,000 broad-based tasks derived from the U.S. Department of Labor O*NET categorization that are text-based and thus LLM-addressable. Based on more than 17,000 evaluations by workers from these jobs, we find little evidence of crashing waves (in contrast to recent work by METR), but substantial evidence that rising tides are the primary form of AI automation. AI performance is high and improving rapidly across a wide range of tasks. We estimate that, in 2024-Q2, AI models successfully complete tasks that take humans approximately 3-4 hours with about a 50% success rate, increasing to about 65% by 2025-Q3. If recent trends in AI capability growth persist, this pace of AI improvement implies that LLMs will be able to complete most text-related tasks with success rates of, on average, 80%-95% by 2029 at a minimally sufficient quality level. Achieving near-perfect success rates at this quality level or comparable success rates at superior quality would require several additional years. These AI capability improvements would impact the economy and labor market as organizations adopt AI, which could have a substantially longer timeline.

</details>


### [136] [CogBias: Measuring and Mitigating Cognitive Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01366)
*Fan Huang,Songheng Zhang,Haewoon Kwak,Jisun An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了LLMs中的认知偏见，开发了LLM CogBias基准测试，发现偏见在模型激活空间中线性可分，并通过激活导向技术成功减少了26-32%的偏见分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地应用于高风险决策场景，虽然已有研究表明LLMs在行为上表现出认知偏见，但这些偏见是否对应可识别的内部表征，以及能否通过针对性干预来缓解，仍然是一个开放性问题。

Method: 定义了LLM认知偏见为系统性的、可重复的与正确答案的偏差，并引入了LLM CogBias基准，围绕四个认知偏见家族（判断、信息处理、社会和反应）组织。使用线性探针在对比设计下分析偏见表征，并应用激活导向技术来调节偏见行为。

Result: 认知偏见在所有四个家族中系统性出现，其程度和去偏见响应强烈依赖于家族：提示级去偏见显著减少了反应偏见，但对判断偏见产生了反效果。偏见在模型激活空间中编码为线性可分的方向。激活导向技术实现了26-32%的偏见分数减少，同时在25个基准测试中保持了下游能力（Llama：性能下降可忽略；Qwen：判断偏见最多下降19.0个百分点）。尽管不同模型间的偏见表征近乎正交（平均余弦相似度0.01），但导向技术在跨架构中减少了相似比例的偏见。

Conclusion: LLMs中的认知偏见是可识别和可调节的，偏见在激活空间中线性可分，激活导向技术能有效减少偏见而不显著损害模型能力。不同模型间偏见表征的差异表明偏见编码具有模型特异性，但功能组织可能共享，这为理解和缓解LLMs中的认知偏见提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making contexts. While prior work has shown that LLMs exhibit cognitive biases behaviorally, whether these biases correspond to identifiable internal representations and can be mitigated through targeted intervention remains an open question. We define LLM cognitive bias as systematic, reproducible deviations from correct answers in tasks with computable ground-truth baselines, and introduce LLM CogBias, a benchmark organized around four families of cognitive biases: Judgment, Information Processing, Social, and Response. We evaluate three LLMs and find that cognitive biases emerge systematically across all four families, with magnitudes and debiasing responses that are strongly family-dependent: prompt-level debiasing substantially reduces Response biases but backfires for Judgment biases. Using linear probes under a contrastive design, we show that these biases are encoded as linearly separable directions in model activation space. Finally, we apply activation steering to modulate biased behavior, achieving 26--32\% reduction in bias score (fraction of biased responses) while preserving downstream capability on 25 benchmarks (Llama: negligible degradation; Qwen: up to $-$19.0pp for Judgment biases). Despite near-orthogonal bias representations across models (mean cosine similarity 0.01), steering reduces bias at similar rates across architectures ($r(246)$=.621, $p$<.001), suggesting shared functional organization.

</details>


### [137] [RIFT: A RubrIc Failure Mode Taxonomy and Automated Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01375)
*Zhengyang Qi,Charles Dickens,Derek Pham,Amanda Dsouza,Armin Parchami,Frederic Sala,Paroma Varma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RIFT分类法，用于系统诊断评分标准的质量问题，包含8种故障模式，分为3大类，并通过自动化指标实现可扩展诊断


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的评估在LLM基准测试和训练中被广泛使用，但缺乏从聚合或下游信号中诊断评分标准质量问题的原则性方法

Method: 使用扎根理论开发RIFT分类法，通过迭代标注来自5个不同基准的评分标准，包含8种故障模式（可靠性故障、内容有效性故障、后果有效性故障），并设计自动化评分标准质量指标

Result: 观察到公平的整体一致性（87%成对一致性和0.64平均Cohen's kappa），自动化指标与人工故障模式标注对齐，达到最高0.86 F1分数

Conclusion: RIFT分类法为诊断评分标准质量问题提供了系统框架，自动化指标支持可扩展的诊断，有助于改进LLM评估和训练中的评分标准设计

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is widely used in LLM benchmarks and training pipelines for open-ended, less verifiable tasks. While prior work has demonstrated the effectiveness of rubrics using downstream signals such as reinforcement learning outcomes, there remains no principled way to diagnose rubric quality issues from such aggregated or downstream signals alone. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT: RubrIc Failure mode Taxonomy, a taxonomy for systematically characterizing failure modes in rubric composition and design. RIFT consists of eight failure modes organized into three high-level categories: Reliability Failures, Content Validity Failures, and Consequential Validity Failures. RIFT is developed using grounded theory by iteratively annotating rubrics drawn from five diverse benchmarks spanning general instruction following, code generation, creative writing, and expert-level deep research, until no new failure modes are identified. We evaluate the consistency of the taxonomy by measuring agreement among independent human annotators, observing fair agreement overall (87% pairwise agreement and 0.64 average Cohen's kappa). Finally, to support scalable diagnosis, we propose automated rubric quality metrics and show that they align with human failure-mode annotations, achieving up to 0.86 F1.

</details>


### [138] [Leveraging the Value of Information in POMDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01434)
*Zakariya Laouar,Qi Heng Ho,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信息价值(VOI)的POMDP规划框架VOIMCP，通过选择性忽略低信息价值的观测来高效分配计算资源，在有限规划时间内获得高性能策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP在处理状态和转移不确定性方面提供了原则性形式化，但在有限规划时间内获得高性能策略仍然面临维数灾难和历史灾难的挑战。许多POMDP问题中，信息价值在信念空间中的分布是不均匀的，这为优化计算资源分配提供了机会。

Method: 提出了一个动态规划框架，根据每个信念点的信息价值有条件地处理观测。基于此框架开发了VOIMCP算法，这是一种蒙特卡洛树搜索算法，当VOI较低时选择性地忽略观测信息，避免不必要的观测分支扩展。

Result: 为VOI推理框架提供了理论上的近优性保证，并为VOIMCP推导了非渐近收敛界。仿真评估表明，VOIMCP在多个POMDP基准测试中优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用信息价值在信念空间中的变化特性，提出的VOIMCP框架能够更高效地分配计算资源，在有限规划时间内获得更好的性能，为解决大规模POMDP的规划时间限制问题提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) offer a principled formalism for planning under state and transition uncertainty. Despite advances made towards solving large POMDPs, obtaining performant policies under limited planning time remains a major challenge due to the curse of dimensionality and the curse of history. For many POMDP problems, the value of information (VOI) - the expected performance gain from reasoning about observations - varies over the belief space. We introduce a dynamic programming framework that exploits this structure by conditionally processing observations based on the value of information at each belief. Building on this framework, we propose Value of Information Monte Carlo planning (VOIMCP), a Monte Carlo Tree Search algorithm that allocates computational effort more efficiently by selectively disregarding observation information when the VOI is low, avoiding unnecessary branching of observations. We provide theoretical guarantees on the near-optimality of our VOI reasoning framework and derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds for VOIMCP. Simulation evaluations demonstrate that VOIMCP outperforms baselines on several POMDP benchmarks.

</details>


### [139] [When AI Gets it Wong: Reliability and Risk in AI-Assisted Medication Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01449)
*Khalid Adnan Alsayed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究AI辅助用药系统的可靠性，重点关注系统错误类型及其临床后果，强调需要超越传统性能指标进行风险评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在医疗和药学领域应用日益广泛，但在高风险用药管理中，即使单个错误也可能导致严重患者伤害。传统评估指标无法充分反映系统在真实决策中的可靠性，需要关注错误发生方式和临床影响

Method: 通过一系列受控模拟场景（包括药物相互作用和剂量决策），分析不同类型的系统故障：漏检相互作用、错误风险标记和不适当剂量推荐

Result: 研究发现AI用药错误可能导致药物不良反应、无效治疗或延误护理，特别是在缺乏足够人工监督的情况下。过度依赖AI推荐和决策过程透明度有限是主要风险

Conclusion: 提出了以可靠性为中心的医疗AI评估视角，强调理解故障行为和真实世界影响的重要性。在安全关键领域如药学实践中，需要将传统性能指标与风险感知评估方法相结合

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly integrated into healthcare and pharmacy workflows, supporting tasks such as medication recommendations, dosage determination, and drug interaction detection. While these systems often demonstrate strong performance under standard evaluation metrics, their reliability in real-world decision-making remains insufficiently understood. In high-risk domains such as medication management, even a single incorrect recommendation can result in severe patient harm. This paper examines the reliability of AI-assisted medication systems by focusing on system failures and their potential clinical consequences. Rather than evaluating performance solely through aggregate metrics, this work shifts attention towards how errors occur and what happens when AI systems produce incorrect outputs. Through a series of controlled, simulated scenarios involving drug interactions and dosage decisions, we analyse different types of system failures, including missed interactions, incorrect risk flagging, and inappropriate dosage recommendations. The findings highlight that AI errors in medication-related contexts can lead to adverse drug reactions, ineffective treatment, or delayed care, particularly when systems are used without sufficient human oversight. Furthermore, the paper discusses the risks of over-reliance on AI recommendations and the challenges posed by limited transparency in decision-making processes. This work contributes a reliability-focused perspective on AI evaluation in healthcare, emphasising the importance of understanding failure behavior and real-world impact. It highlights the need to complement traditional performance metrics with risk-aware evaluation approaches, particularly in safety-critical domains such as pharmacy practice.

</details>


### [140] [A Multi-Agent Human-LLM Collaborative Framework for Closed-Loop Scientific Literature Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01452)
*Maxwell J. Jacobson,Daniel Xie,Jackson Shen,Adil Wazeer,Haiyan Wang,Xinghang Zhang,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Elhuyar是一个多智能体、人机协同系统，结合LLM、结构化AI和人类科学家，从科学文献中提取、分析和迭代精炼见解，应用于材料科学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现因文献碎片化而受阻，现有AI工具缺乏结构化、多步骤的方法来从科学文献中提取深度见解，LLM存在幻觉和不完整提取问题。

Method: 多智能体人机协同框架，分配任务给专门智能体进行论文筛选、数据提取、模型拟合和结果总结，人类监督确保可靠性，生成结构化报告（数据、可视化、模型方程、文本摘要），支持迭代精炼。

Result: 在材料科学中应用于钨在氦离子辐照下的文献分析，发现氦气泡生长与辐照剂量和温度呈指数相关，为聚变反应堆等离子体面向材料提供见解。

Conclusion: AI辅助的文献综述能够揭示科学模式并加速发现，Elhuyar系统展示了结合LLM、结构化AI和人类监督的多智能体方法的有效性。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is slowed by fragmented literature that requires excessive human effort to gather, analyze, and understand. AI tools, including autonomous summarization and question answering, have been developed to aid in understanding scientific literature. However, these tools lack the structured, multi-step approach necessary for extracting deep insights from scientific literature. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new possibilities for literature analysis, but remain unreliable due to hallucinations and incomplete extraction. We introduce Elhuyar, a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop system that integrates LLMs, structured AI, and human scientists to extract, analyze, and iteratively refine insights from scientific literature. The framework distributes tasks among specialized agents for filtering papers, extracting data, fitting models, and summarizing findings, with human oversight ensuring reliability. The system generates structured reports with extracted data, visualizations, model equations, and text summaries, enabling deeper inquiry through iterative refinement. Deployed in materials science, it analyzed literature on tungsten under helium-ion irradiation, showing experimentally correlated exponential helium bubble growth with irradiation dose and temperature, offering insight for plasma-facing materials (PFMs) in fusion reactors. This demonstrates how AI-assisted literature review can uncover scientific patterns and accelerate discovery.

</details>


### [141] [Infeasibility Aware Large Language Models for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01455)
*Yakun Wang,Min Chen,Zeguan Wu,Junyu Liu,Sitao Zhang,Zhenwen Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个不可行性感知框架，结合可验证数据集构建、监督微调和LLM辅助下游搜索，用于组合优化问题，特别针对minor-embedding问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法主要关注可行实例的解决方案生成，缺乏对不可行性检测的明确处理，需要一种能够同时处理可行和不可行实例的框架

Method: 1) 提出不可行性感知框架；2) 针对minor-embedding问题引入新的数学规划公式和可证明的零阶段不可行性筛选；3) 构建可验证的训练数据集；4) 对8B参数LLM进行监督微调；5) 利用LLM输出作为下游局部搜索的初始解

Result: 微调模型比GPT-5.2整体准确率提升高达30%；LLM引导的初始解在下游局部搜索中相比从头开始提供高达2倍加速

Conclusion: 该框架成功将LLM应用于组合优化问题，不仅提高了解决方案生成质量，还增强了不可行性检测能力，并通过LLM辅助加速了下游优化过程

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored for NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems, but most existing methods emphasize feasible-instance solution generation and do not explicitly address infeasibility detection. We propose an infeasibility-aware framework that combines certifiable dataset construction, supervised fine-tuning, and LLM-assisted downstream search. For the minor-embedding problem, we introduce a new mathematical programming formulation together with provable zero-phase infeasibility screening, which enables scalable construction of training instances labeled either as feasible with structured certificates or as certifiably infeasible. Using training data generated through this exact optimization pipeline, we show that an 8B-parameter LLM can be fine-tuned to jointly perform solution generation and infeasibility detection. We further utilize LLM outputs as warm starts for downstream local search, providing a practical way to accelerate optimization even when the LLM outputs are imperfect. Experiments show that our fine-tuned model improves overall accuracy by up to 30\% over GPT-5.2; meanwhile LLM-guided warm starts provide up to $2\times$ speedup compared with starting from scratch in downstream local search.

</details>


### [142] [Reducing Hallucinations in LLM-based Scientific Literature Analysis Using Peer Context Outlier Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01461)
*Daniel Xie,Maxwell J. Jacobson,Adil Wazeer,Haiyan Wang,Xinghang Zhang,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: P-COD是一种利用文档间关系检测异常值的新方法，通过对比提取数据与语料库中已验证的同行信息，调整置信度分数并标记低置信度结果，从而减少LLM在科学文献摘要中的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前减少LLM幻觉的方法（如提示工程、思维链提示）主要关注单个文档，未能考虑语料库中文档间的关系。在科学文献摘要领域，具有相似实验设置的论文应得出相似结论，但现有方法无法有效利用这种关系来提高数据提取的准确性。

Method: 提出Peer Context Outlier Detection (P-COD)方法：1）利用文档间关系改进提取准确性；2）将提取的数据与语料库中已验证的同行信息进行比较；3）调整置信度分数；4）标记低置信度结果供专家审查；5）将得到同行验证的高置信度结果视为可靠。

Result: 实验表明，在6个科学领域中，异常值检测精度高达98%。该方法能有效减少幻觉，增强对自动化系统的信任，让研究人员专注于模糊案例，从而简化数据提取工作流程。

Conclusion: P-COD通过利用文档间关系进行同行验证，显著提高了LLM在科学文献数据提取中的准确性，减少了幻觉，为研究人员提供了更可靠、高效的自动化数据提取解决方案。

Abstract: Reducing hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) is essential for improving the accuracy of data extraction from large text corpora. Current methods, like prompt engineering and chain-of-thought prompting, focus on individual documents but fail to consider relationships across a corpus. This paper introduces Peer Context Outlier Detection (P-COD), a novel approach that uses the relationships between documents to improve extraction accuracy. Our application domain is in scientific literature summarization, where papers with similar experiment settings should draw similar conclusions. By comparing extracted data to validated peer information within the corpus, we adjust confidence scores and flag low-confidence results for expert review. High-confidence results, supported by peer validation, are considered reliable. Our experiments demonstrate up to 98% precision in outlier detection across 6 domains of science, demonstrating that our design reduces hallucinations, enhances trust in automated systems, and allows researchers to focus on ambiguous cases, streamlining the data extraction workflows.

</details>


### [143] [A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Metasurface Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01480)
*Yi Huang,Bowen Zheng,Yunxi Dong,Hong Tang,Huan Zhao,S. M. Rakibul Hasan Shawon,Hualiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个基于技能演化的智能体框架，用于超表面逆向设计，通过迭代优化特定求解器策略来积累可重用工作流知识，无需修改模型权重或物理求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超表面逆向设计需要计算电磁学和求解器特定软件工程的专业知识，现有语言驱动系统缺乏跨任务可重用工作流知识的保存能力，限制了设计流程的自主性和可访问性。

Method: 提出一个智能体框架，包含编码智能体、演化技能工件和基于物理仿真的确定性评估器，通过迭代精炼特定求解器策略实现跨任务技能演化，不修改模型权重或底层物理求解器。

Result: 在分布内任务中，技能演化将任务成功率从38%提升至74%，通过标准分数从0.510提升至0.870，平均尝试次数从4.10降至2.30；在分布外任务中，成功率变化不大，但最佳边界改进、错误组成变化和智能体行为表明工作流知识的部分迁移。

Conclusion: 技能演化的主要价值在于围绕可靠计算引擎积累可重用的特定求解器专业知识，为实现更自主、更易访问的超表面逆向设计工作流提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Metasurface inverse design has become central to realizing complex optical functionality, yet translating target responses into executable, solver-compatible workflows still demands specialized expertise in computational electromagnetics and solver-specific software engineering. Recent large language models (LLMs) offer a complementary route to reducing this workflow-construction burden, but existing language-driven systems remain largely session-bounded and do not preserve reusable workflow knowledge across inverse-design tasks. We present an agentic framework for metasurface inverse design that addresses this limitation through context-level skill evolution. The framework couples a coding agent, evolving skill artifacts, and a deterministic evaluator grounded in physical simulation so that solver-specific strategies can be iteratively refined across tasks without modifying model weights or the underlying physics solver. We evaluate the framework on a benchmark spanning multiple metasurface inverse-design task types, with separate training-aligned and held-out task families. Evolved skills raise in-distribution task success from 38% to 74%, increase criteria pass fraction from 0.510 to 0.870, and reduce average attempts from 4.10 to 2.30. On held-out task families, binary success changes only marginally, but improvements in best margin together with shifts in error composition and agent behavior indicate partial transfer of workflow knowledge. These results suggest that the main value of skill evolution lies in accumulating reusable solver-specific expertise around reliable computational engines, thereby offering a practical path toward more autonomous and accessible metasurface inverse-design workflows.

</details>


### [144] [A Role-Based LLM Framework for Structured Information Extraction from Healthy Food Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01529)
*Congjing Zhang,Ruoxuan Bao,Jingyu Li,Yoav Ackerman,Shuai Huang,Yanfang Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于角色分工的LLM框架，通过分配专业角色（政策分析师、法律策略专家、食品系统专家）来改进健康食品政策信息提取，解决传统方法中的幻觉、误分类和遗漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在健康食品政策信息提取中存在幻觉、误分类和遗漏等问题，这些问题源于政策文档的结构多样性和不一致性。需要一种更可靠、透明的自动化方法来提高信息提取的准确性和一致性。

Method: 提出基于角色的LLM框架，分配三个专业角色：LLM政策分析师负责元数据和机制分类，LLM法律策略专家识别复杂法律方法，LLM食品系统专家分类食品系统阶段。框架将结构化领域知识（法律机制定义和分类标准）融入角色特定提示中，模拟专家分析工作流程。

Result: 在Healthy Food Policy Project数据库的608个健康食品政策上评估，使用Llama-3.3-70B模型，与零样本、少样本和思维链基线比较。提出的框架在复杂推理任务中表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 基于角色的LLM框架为健康政策信息提取提供了可靠、透明的自动化方法，在复杂推理任务中优于传统基线方法，能够有效减少幻觉、误分类和遗漏问题。

Abstract: Current Large Language Model (LLM) approaches for information extraction (IE) in the healthy food policy domain are often hindered by various factors, including misinformation, specifically hallucinations, misclassifications, and omissions that result from the structural diversity and inconsistency of policy documents. To address these limitations, this study proposes a role-based LLM framework that automates the IE from unstructured policy data by assigning specialized roles: an LLM policy analyst for metadata and mechanism classification, an LLM legal strategy specialist for identifying complex legal approaches, and an LLM food system expert for categorizing food system stages. This framework mimics expert analysis workflows by incorporating structured domain knowledge, including explicit definitions of legal mechanisms and classification criteria, into role-specific prompts. We evaluate the framework using 608 healthy food policies from the Healthy Food Policy Project (HFPP) database, comparing its performance against zero-shot, few-shot, and chain-of-thought (CoT) baselines using Llama-3.3-70B. Our proposed framework demonstrates superior performance in complex reasoning tasks, offering a reliable and transparent methodology for automating IE from health policies.

</details>


### [145] [RAE-AR: Taming Autoregressive Models with Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01545)
*Hu Yu,Hang Xu,Jie Huang,Zeyue Xue,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探索将表示自编码器（RAE）整合到连续自回归（AR）生成模型中，解决了高维表示在AR范式下的建模挑战，通过分布归一化和噪声注入方法，使RAE-AR达到与VAE相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VAE编码器长期主导生成模型的潜在空间，而预训练表示编码器（如DINO、SigLIP、MAE）的潜在表示被认为不适合生成建模。虽然RAE方法显示了表示自编码器在生成任务中的潜力，但其与连续自回归模型的整合仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出RAE-AR框架，针对AR模型特性解决两大挑战：1）通过分布归一化进行标记简化，降低复杂标记分布建模难度；2）在训练时引入高斯噪声注入，缓解高维表示带来的训练-推理差距（暴露偏差）。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法显著缩小了性能差距，使表示自编码器在AR模型上能够达到与传统VAE相当的结果，为视觉理解和生成建模的统一架构铺平道路。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将表示自编码器整合到连续自回归生成模型中，通过针对性的技术改进解决了高维表示在AR范式下的特有挑战，实现了表示编码器在生成任务中的有效应用。

Abstract: The latent space of generative modeling is long dominated by the VAE encoder. The latents from the pretrained representation encoders (e.g., DINO, SigLIP, MAE) are previously considered inappropriate for generative modeling. Recently, RAE method lights the hope and reveals that the representation autoencoder can also achieve competitive performance as the VAE encoder. However, the integration of representation autoencoder into continuous autoregressive (AR) models, remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate the challenges of employing high-dimensional representation autoencoders within the AR paradigm, denoted as \textit{RAE-AR}. We focus on the unique properties of AR models and identify two primary hurdles: complex token-wise distribution modeling and the high-dimensionality amplified training-inference gap (exposure bias). To address these, we introduce token simplification via distribution normalization to ease modeling difficulty and improve convergence. Furthermore, we enhance prediction robustness by incorporating Gaussian noise injection during training to mitigate exposure bias. Our empirical results demonstrate that these modifications substantially bridge the performance gap, enabling representation autoencoder to achieve results comparable to traditional VAEs on AR models. This work paves the way for a more unified architecture across visual understanding and generative modeling.

</details>


### [146] [Does Your Optimizer Care How You Normalize? Normalization-Optimizer Coupling in LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01563)
*Abdelrahman Abouzeid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM训练中归一化层与优化器之间存在显著交互效应：Derf归一化与Muon优化器组合时性能显著下降，而DyT归一化则无此问题，表明需将归一化与优化器作为协同设计而非独立选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM训练中将归一化层和优化器视为独立设计选择，但本研究通过实验发现这种假设可能不成立，特定组合（Derf+Muon）存在显著的负交互效应，需要重新审视两者的协同设计。

Method: 采用3x2因子设计实验，在1B参数规模、1000训练步数下测试不同归一化层（Derf、RMSNorm、DyT）与优化器（AdamW、Muon）的组合，分析性能差异和交互效应，并通过EMA混合和alpha参数调整进行修复。

Result: Derf与Muon组合存在严重负交互效应：性能差距从AdamW下的+0.31 nats扩大到Muon下的+0.97 nats（约3倍）。DyT作为有界归一化对照组无此问题。失败原因包括饱和效应和尺度盲区，通过EMA混合恢复约84%性能，降低alpha参数恢复约80%性能。

Conclusion: 归一化层与优化器不能视为独立设计，特定组合存在显著交互效应。Derf与Muon组合的失败容易被短时试验忽略，需通过参数调整（alpha降低至0.3）或EMA混合来缓解。这强调了LLM训练中组件协同设计的重要性。

Abstract: In LLM training, normalization layers and optimizers are typically treated as independent design choices. In a 3x2 factorial at 1B parameters and 1000 training steps, we show this assumption can fail: Dynamic Erf (Derf; Chen & Liu, 2025) suffers a large negative interaction with Muon (Jordan, 2024), with its gap to RMSNorm growing from +0.31 nats under AdamW to +0.97 under Muon, approximately three times larger. Dynamic Tanh (DyT; Zhu et al., 2025), included as a bounded-normalizer control, shows no such penalty. Our evidence points to two failure modes of erf under Muon's faster spectral-norm growth: saturation (lossy compression) and scale blindness (discarding activation magnitude). An EMA-blend that reintroduces running scale estimates recovers ~84% of the gap. Separately, reducing Derf's alpha from its published default (0.5 to 0.3) recovers ~80% by keeping erf in its near-linear regime, where it approximately preserves relative scale; this setting is not the published default of Chen & Liu (2025). Using Derf's published default alpha with Muon incurs a 0.66-nat interaction penalty without producing NaNs or divergence, making the failure easy to miss in short pilot runs.

</details>


### [147] [NED-Tree: Bridging the Semantic Gap with Nonlinear Element Decomposition Tree for LLM Nonlinear Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01588)
*Zhijing Hu,Yufan Deng,Haoyang Liu,Changjun Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NED-Tree框架通过句子级提取和递归树结构解决LLM在非线性运筹学问题建模中的语义对齐问题，在10个基准测试中达到72.51%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在线性运筹学问题建模中表现良好，但在现实世界非线性场景中因数学公式与求解器代码之间的语义不对齐以及信息提取不稳定而性能严重下降，需要解决这一关键挑战。

Method: 提出NED-Tree系统框架：采用句子级提取策略确保参数映射和可追溯性；使用递归树结构自适应地将复杂非线性项分解为求解器兼容的子元素；同时创建NEXTOR基准测试专门用于复杂非线性、多约束运筹学问题。

Result: 在10个基准测试中，NED-Tree达到72.51%的平均准确率，建立了新的最先进水平，首次通过元素分解驱动LLM解决非线性建模困难，实现了建模语义与代码语义的对齐。

Conclusion: NED-Tree通过系统化方法有效解决了LLM在非线性运筹学问题建模中的语义对齐挑战，为自动化自然语言到可执行模型的翻译提供了可靠框架，相关资源和基准测试已公开可用。

Abstract: Automating the translation of Operations Research (OR) problems from natural language to executable models is a critical challenge. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in linear tasks, they suffer from severe performance degradation in real-world nonlinear scenarios due to semantic misalignment between mathematical formulations and solver codes, as well as unstable information extraction. In this study, we introduce NED-Tree, a systematic framework designed to bridge the semantic gap. NED-Tree employs (a) a sentence-by-sentence extraction strategy to ensure robust parameter mapping and traceability; and (b) a recursive tree-based structure that adaptively decomposes complex nonlinear terms into solver-compatible sub-elements. Additionally, we present NEXTOR, a novel benchmark specifically designed for complex nonlinear, extensive-constraint OR problems. Experiments across 10 benchmarks demonstrate that NED-Tree establishes a new state-of-the-art with 72.51% average accuracy, NED-Tree is the first framework that drives LLMs to resolve nonlinear modeling difficulties through element decomposition, achieving alignment between modeling semantics and code semantics. The NED-Tree framework and benchmark are accessible in the anonymous repository https://anonymous.4open.science/r/NORA-NEXTOR.

</details>


### [148] [ThinkTwice: Jointly Optimizing Large Language Models for Reasoning and Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01591)
*Difan Jiao,Qianfeng Wen,Blair Yang,Zhenwei Tang,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkTwice是一个两阶段框架，通过GRPO联合优化LLM解决推理问题和精炼答案，无需正确性信号或批判性标注，在数学推理基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习与验证推理（RLVR）方法通常需要额外的正确性信号或批判性标注来训练自我精炼能力，这限制了方法的可扩展性和实用性。ThinkTwice旨在开发一个无需这些额外监督信号的联合训练框架。

Method: ThinkTwice采用简单的两阶段框架：1）首先优化模型解决推理问题；2）然后优化模型精炼自己的解决方案。两个阶段使用相同的二元正确性奖励，基于Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行训练，无需正确性信号或批判性标注。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准和两个模型家族（Qwen3-4B和Olmo3-7B）上，ThinkTwice显著优于竞争性的在线策略优化基线。在Qwen3-4B上，ThinkTwice在AIME基准上比GRPO在精炼前提升5个百分点，在一次自我精炼后提升11.5个百分点（pass@4）。训练动态分析显示存在隐式的"纠正-强化"课程：早期精炼主要纠正错误，随着模型改进自然转向保护已正确的解决方案。

Conclusion: ThinkTwice建立了推理和自我精炼的联合训练作为一种原则性且有效的RLVR方法，展示了无需额外监督信号的自我精炼训练的可行性，并为更高效的语言模型训练提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce ThinkTwice, a simple two-phase framework that jointly optimizes LLMs to solve reasoning problems and refine the answers, based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). In each pair of training steps, ThinkTwice first optimizes the model on solving reasoning problems, then optimizes it on refining its own solutions to the same problems, using the same binary correctness reward in both phases without correctness signals or critique annotations. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and two model families including Qwen3-4B and Olmo3-7B, ThinkTwice substantially improves both reasoning and refinement performance over competitive online policy optimization baselines. Specifically, on Qwen3-4B, ThinkTwice outperforms GRPO on AIME by 5 percentage points before refinement and by 11.5 points after one self-refinement step, measured by pass@4. Analysis of the training dynamics of ThinkTwice reveals an implicit rectify-then-fortify curriculum: refinement predominantly corrects errors early in training and naturally shifts toward preserving already-correct solutions as the model improves, yielding a more rectified reward signal. Our work establishes joint training of reasoning and self-refinement as a principled and effective methodology for RLVR.

</details>


### [149] [Do Large Language Models Mentalize When They Teach?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01594)
*Sevan K. Harootonian,Mark K. Ho,Thomas L. Griffiths,Yael Niv,Ilia Sucholutsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs作为教师时主要采用贝叶斯最优教学策略（通过推理学习者知识），而非简单启发式规则，其教学决策与人类相似，但提示干预不一定能改善教学效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLMs在教学决策时是采用基于学习者知识推理的复杂策略，还是使用简单的启发式规则，通过受控任务比较LLMs与人类的教学策略

Method: 使用先前研究人类教学策略的受控任务：教师LLM观察学习者在奖励标注有向图中的轨迹，揭示单条边以改善学习者路径规划。将LLMs作为模拟教师，用与人类研究相同的认知模型（贝叶斯最优教师、弱贝叶斯变体、启发式基线、非心理化效用模型）拟合其选择。进行基线实验和支架干预实验

Result: 在基线实验中，大多数LLMs表现良好，策略变化小，图间表现与人类相似。模型比较显示贝叶斯最优教学最能解释多数模型的选择。支架干预中，模型遵循推理或奖励导向的提示，但这些干预在启发式不一致测试图上不一定改善教学，有时反而降低性能

Conclusion: 认知模型拟合揭示了LLMs的教学策略，表明它们主要采用贝叶斯最优教学策略而非简单启发式。提示合规性不能保证更好的教学决策，支架干预不一定改善教学效果

Abstract: How do LLMs decide what to teach next: by reasoning about a learner's knowledge, or by using simpler rules of thumb? We test this in a controlled task previously used to study human teaching strategies. On each trial, a teacher LLM sees a hypothetical learner's trajectory through a reward-annotated directed graph and must reveal a single edge so the learner would choose a better path if they replanned. We run a range of LLMs as simulated teachers and fit their trial-by-trial choices with the same cognitive models used for humans: a Bayes-Optimal teacher that infers which transitions the learner is missing (inverse planning), weaker Bayesian variants, heuristic baselines (e.g., reward based), and non-mentalizing utility models. In a baseline experiment matched to the stimuli presented to human subjects, most LLMs perform well, show little change in strategy over trials, and their graph-by-graph performance is similar to that of humans. Model comparison (BIC) shows that Bayes-Optimal teaching best explains most models' choices. When given a scaffolding intervention, models follow auxiliary inference- or reward-focused prompts, but these scaffolds do not reliably improve later teaching on heuristic-incongruent test graphs and can sometimes reduce performance. Overall, cognitive model fits provide insight into LLM tutoring policies and show that prompt compliance does not guarantee better teaching decisions.

</details>


### [150] [MM-ReCoder: Advancing Chart-to-Code Generation with Reinforcement Learning and Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01600)
*Zitian Tang,Xu Zhang,Jianbo Yuan,Yang Zou,Varad Gunjal,Songyao Jiang,Davide Modolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MM-ReCoder：一种基于强化学习和自校正能力的图表转代码生成模型，采用两阶段多轮自校正RL策略，在三个基准测试中实现最先进性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在图表转代码任务中主要依赖监督微调，缺乏代码执行环境暴露，且即使最先进的模型也难以有效进行自校正。需要一种能够通过执行反馈自我改进的强化学习方法。

Method: 提出MM-ReCoder模型，采用基于组相对策略优化的两阶段多轮自校正强化学习策略。第一阶段通过共享首轮增强模型自校正能力，第二阶段通过全轨迹优化提升编码能力。模型通过与环境的交互和迭代自校正来学习生成更准确、可执行的代码。

Result: 在三个图表转代码基准测试中，MM-ReCoder实现了最先进的性能，能够生成更准确且可执行的代码。

Conclusion: MM-ReCoder通过强化学习和自校正机制有效解决了现有方法在图表转代码任务中的局限性，为多模态编码任务提供了一种新的训练范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently demonstrated promising capabilities in multimodal coding tasks such as chart-to-code generation. However, existing methods primarily rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which requires the model to learn code patterns through chart-code pairs but does not expose the model to a code execution environment. Moreover, while self-correction through execution feedback offers a potential route to improve coding quality, even state-of-the-art MLLMs have been shown to struggle with effective self-correction. In this work, we introduce MM-ReCoder, a chart-to-code generation model trained with reinforcement learning (RL) and equipped with self-correction ability. We propose a two-stage multi-turn self-correction RL strategy based on Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). The first stage enhances the model's self-correction ability via rolling out a shared first turn, while the second stage improves the coding capability with full-trajectory optimization. MM-ReCoder learns to produce more accurate and executable code through the interaction with the environment and by iteratively correcting its own outputs. Our results on three chart-to-code benchmarks demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of MM-ReCoder.

</details>


### [151] [CRaFT: Circuit-Guided Refusal Feature Selection via Cross-Layer Transcoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01604)
*Su-Hyeon Kim,Hyundong Jin,Yejin Lee,Yo-Sub Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRaFT：基于电路引导的拒绝特征选择框架，通过边界附近提示评估特征对拒绝-合规决策的影响，相比基于激活强度的基线方法显著提升攻击成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拒绝特征选择方法依赖特征在有害提示上的激活强度，这倾向于捕捉表面信号而非拒绝决策的因果因素。需要更可靠的方法来识别因果介导拒绝行为的特征。

Method: 提出CRaFT框架，通过评估特征在拒绝边界附近提示上对模型拒绝-合规决策的影响来排名特征，使用电路引导方法识别因果相关的拒绝特征。

Result: 在Gemma-3-1B-it模型上，CRaFT将攻击成功率从6.7%提升至48.2%，在多个越狱基准测试中优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 电路影响是比激活强度更可靠的标准，用于识别因果介导拒绝行为的特征，这有助于更深入理解LLM拒绝行为的内在机制。

Abstract: As safety concerns around large language models (LLMs) grow, understanding the internal mechanisms underlying refusal behavior has become increasingly important. Recent work has studied this behavior by identifying internal features associated with refusal and manipulating them to induce compliance with harmful requests. However, existing refusal feature selection methods rely on how strongly features activate on harmful prompts, which tends to capture superficial signals rather than the causal factors underlying the refusal decision. We propose CRaFT, a circuit-guided refusal feature selection framework that ranks features by their influence on the model's refusal-compliance decision using prompts near the refusal boundary. On Gemma-3-1B-it, CRaFT improves attack success rate (ASR) from 6.7% to 48.2% and outperforms baseline methods across multiple jailbreak benchmarks. These results suggest that circuit influence is a more reliable criterion than activation magnitude for identifying features that causally mediate refusal behavior.

</details>


### [152] [From Multi-Agent to Single-Agent: When Is Skill Distillation Beneficial?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01608)
*Binyan Xu,Dong Fang,Haitao Li,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Metric Freedom (F)作为技能效用的先验预测器，通过度量评估指标的拓扑刚性来预测多智能体系统蒸馏为单智能体技能的效果，并基于此设计自适应蒸馏框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统(MAS)虽然能处理复杂任务，但存在协调开销大、上下文碎片化和脆弱的阶段排序等问题。将MAS蒸馏为单智能体技能可以绕过这些成本，但缺乏原则性指导来确定何时蒸馏以及蒸馏什么，导致实证结果不一致（技能提升从28%改进到2%退化）。

Method: 1. 引入Metric Freedom (F)作为技能效用的先验预测器，通过Mantel测试量化输出多样性与得分方差之间的耦合关系，测量评估指标评分景观的拓扑刚性。2. 提出两阶段自适应蒸馏框架：第一阶段作为选择性提取机制，在"自由"指标上提取工具和知识同时丢弃限制性结构以保持探索；第二阶段专门针对"刚性"指标(F ≤ 0.6)进行计算密集的迭代优化以消除轨迹局部过拟合。

Result: 在4个任务、11个数据集和6个指标上的评估显示，F能强烈预测技能效用(ρ = -0.62, p < 0.05)。相同的智能体轨迹在刚性指标与自由指标下产生截然相反的技能提升效果，证明技能效用本质上是度量层面的属性。基于此信号的自适应智能体匹配或超越原始MAS，同时将成本降低高达8倍，延迟降低高达15倍。

Conclusion: 技能效用不是由任务决定的，而是由评估指标决定的。Metric Freedom (F)作为第一个先验预测器，能够有效预测技能蒸馏的效果。基于F指导的自适应蒸馏框架能够在保持或提升性能的同时显著降低多智能体系统的协调成本和延迟。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) tackle complex tasks by distributing expertise, though this often comes at the cost of heavy coordination overhead, context fragmentation, and brittle phase ordering. Distilling a MAS into a single-agent skill can bypass these costs, but this conversion lacks a principled answer for when and what to distill. Instead, the empirical outcome is surprisingly inconsistent: skill lift ranges from a 28% improvement to a 2% degradation across metrics of the exact same task. In this work, we reveal that skill utility is governed not by the task, but by the evaluation metric. We introduce Metric Freedom ($F$), the first a priori predictor of skill utility. $F$ measures the topological rigidity of a metric's scoring landscape by quantifying how output diversity couples with score variance via a Mantel test. Guided by $F$, we propose a two-stage adaptive distillation framework. Stage 1 acts as a selective extraction mechanism, extracting tools and knowledge while discarding restrictive structures on "free" metrics to preserve exploration. Stage 2 targets computationally intensive iterative refinement exclusively toward "rigid" metrics ($F \lesssim 0.6$) to eliminate trajectory-local overfitting. Evaluating across 4 tasks, 11 datasets, and 6 metrics, $F$ strongly predicts skill utility ($ρ= -0.62$, $p < 0.05$). Strikingly, identical agent trajectories yield diametrically opposite skill lifts under rigid versus free metrics, demonstrating that skill utility is fundamentally a metric-level property. Driven by this signal, our adaptive agent matches or exceeds the original MAS while reducing cost up to 8$\times$ and latency by up to 15$\times$.

</details>


### [153] [GraphWalk: Enabling Reasoning in Large Language Models through Tool-Based Graph Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01610)
*Taraneh Ghandi,Hamidreza Mahyar,Shachar Klaiman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphWalk：一种无需训练、基于工具的框架，使现成LLM能够通过顺序图导航进行推理，显著提升大规模知识图谱上的复杂查询性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级知识图谱规模庞大，无法放入现有最大上下文窗口，传统基于提示或检索增强生成的方法在处理复杂多跳推理查询时面临严重限制

Method: GraphWalk为LLM提供一组最小正交图操作（工具），使模型能够通过顺序图导航遍历任何图结构，每个工具调用代表可验证的步骤，形成透明执行轨迹

Result: 基于工具的图遍历在所有测试模型家族中相比上下文基线获得显著且一致的性能提升，随着规模增加增益更加明显，而上下文方法在大型图谱上完全失效

Conclusion: GraphWalk证明了通过最小工具集使LLM进行结构化图推理的可行性，为处理企业级知识图谱的复杂查询提供了可扩展的解决方案

Abstract: The use of knowledge graphs for grounding agents in real-world Q&A applications has become increasingly common. Answering complex queries often requires multi-hop reasoning and the ability to navigate vast relational structures. Standard approaches rely on prompting techniques that steer large language models to reason over raw graph context, or retrieval-augmented generation pipelines where relevant subgraphs are injected into the context. These, however, face severe limitations with enterprise-scale KGs that cannot fit in even the largest context windows available today. We present GraphWalk, a problem-agnostic, training-free, tool-based framework that allows off-the-shelf LLMs to reason through sequential graph navigation, dramatically increasing performance across different tasks. Unlike task-specific agent frameworks that encode domain knowledge into specialized tools, GraphWalk equips the LLM with a minimal set of orthogonal graph operations sufficient to traverse any graph structure. We evaluate whether models equipped with GraphWalk can compose these operations into correct multi-step reasoning chains, where each tool call represents a verifiable step creating a transparent execution trace. We first demonstrate our approach on maze traversal, a problem non-reasoning models are completely unable to solve, then present results on graphs resembling real-world enterprise knowledge graphs. To isolate structural reasoning from world knowledge, we evaluate on entirely synthetic graphs with random, non-semantic labels. Our benchmark spans 12 query templates from basic retrieval to compound first-order logic queries. Results show that tool-based traversal yields substantial and consistent gains over in-context baselines across all model families tested, with gains becoming more pronounced as scale increases, precisely where in-context approaches fail catastrophically.

</details>


### [154] [Analysis of LLM Performance on AWS Bedrock: Receipt-item Categorisation Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01615)
*Gabby Sanchez,Sneha Oommen,Cassandra T. Britto,Di Wang,Jung-De Chiou,Maria Spichkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统评估AWS Bedrock上四种指令调优LLM在收据项目分类中的性能与成本，发现Claude 3.7 Sonnet在准确性和成本效率间达到最佳平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在面向生产的分类框架中，需要系统评估大型语言模型在收据项目分类任务上的表现，同时考虑性能（准确性、响应稳定性）和成本（token级别成本），并探索零样本与少样本提示方法在准确性和成本方面的适用性

Method: 在AWS Bedrock平台上比较四种指令调优模型：Claude 3.7 Sonnet、Claude 4 Sonnet、Mixtral 8x7B Instruct和Mistral 7B Instruct，评估指标包括分类准确性、响应稳定性和token级别成本，同时研究零样本和少样本提示方法的效果

Result: 实验结果表明，Claude 3.7 Sonnet在分类准确性和成本效率之间实现了最有利的平衡

Conclusion: Claude 3.7 Sonnet是收据项目分类任务中性能与成本权衡的最佳选择，为生产环境中的LLM应用提供了实用的指导

Abstract: This paper presents a systematic, cost-aware evaluation of large language models (LLMs) for receipt-item categorisation within a production-oriented classification framework. We compare four instruction-tuned models available through AWS Bedrock: Claude 3.7 Sonnet, Claude 4 Sonnet, Mixtral 8x7B Instruct, and Mistral 7B Instruct. The aim of the study was (1) to assess performance across accuracy, response stability, and token-level cost, and (2) to investigate what prompting methods, zero-shot or few-shot, are especially appropriate both in terms of accuracy and in terms of incurred costs. Results of our experiments demonstrated that Claude 3.7 Sonnet achieves the most favourable balance between classification accuracy and cost efficiency.

</details>


### [155] [OSCAR: Orchestrated Self-verification and Cross-path Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01624)
*Yash Shah,Abhijit Chakraborty,Naresh Kumar Devulapally,Vishnu Lokhande,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSCAR：基于扩散语言模型轨迹的推理时幻觉缓解框架，通过并行去噪链检测不确定性位置并进行针对性重掩码


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型提供去噪轨迹作为模型原生信号，理想的幻觉缓解应利用此信号而非依赖外部训练的幻觉分类器。现有方法缺乏对去噪轨迹中不确定性定位的系统研究。

Method: 提出承诺不确定性定位：通过N条并行去噪链计算跨链香农熵，识别不确定性超过无监督阈值的token位置。OSCAR框架包含不确定性检测和基于检索证据的针对性重掩码两个核心组件。

Result: 在TriviaQA、HotpotQA、RAGTruth和CommonsenseQA数据集上，使用LLaDA-8B和Dream-7B模型，OSCAR显著减少幻觉内容并提高事实准确性。跨链发散度指标显示其优于专用训练检测器。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型具有识别事实不确定性的内在能力，OSCAR框架通过不确定性定位和针对性重掩码有效缓解幻觉，为DLM中的定位和不确定性感知生成研究提供基础。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) expose their denoising trajectories, offering a natural handle for inference-time control; accordingly, an ideal hallucination mitigation framework should intervene during generation using this model-native signal rather than relying on an externally trained hallucination classifier. Toward this, we formulate commitment uncertainty localization: given a denoising trajectory, identify token positions whose cross-chain entropy exceeds an unsupervised threshold before factually unreliable commitments propagate into self-consistent but incorrect outputs. We introduce a suite of trajectory-level assessments, including a cross-chain divergence-at-hallucination (CDH) metric, for principled comparison of localization methods. We also introduce OSCAR, a training-free inference-time framework operationalizing this formulation. OSCAR runs N parallel denoising chains with randomized reveal orders, computes cross-chain Shannon entropy to detect high-uncertainty positions, and then performs targeted remasking conditioned on retrieved evidence. Ablations confirm that localization and correction contribute complementary gains, robust across N in {4, 8, 16}. On TriviaQA, HotpotQA, RAGTruth, and CommonsenseQA using LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B, OSCAR enhances generation quality by significantly reducing hallucinated content and improving factual accuracy through uncertainty-guided remasking, which also facilitates more effective integration of retrieved evidence. Its native entropy-based uncertainty signal surpasses that of specialized trained detectors, highlighting an inherent capacity of diffusion language models to identify factual uncertainty that is not present in the sequential token commitment structure of autoregressive models. We are releasing the codebase1 to support future research on localization and uncertainty-aware generation in DLMs.

</details>


### [156] [Exploring Robust Multi-Agent Workflows for Environmental Data Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01647)
*Boyuan Guan,Jason Liu,Yanzhao Wu,Kiavash Bahreini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnviSmart是一个面向环境FAIR数据管理的生产系统，通过三轨知识架构和角色分离的多智能体设计，在LLM驱动的概率性工作流中实现可靠性，防止错误传播到不可逆操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM驱动的智能体嵌入环境FAIR数据管理具有吸引力，但概率性工作流可能产生看似合理但错误的输出，这些错误可能通过表面检查并传播到DOI注册和公开发布等不可逆操作中，需要新的可靠性机制。

Method: EnviSmart采用两种机制：1) 三轨知识架构，将行为（治理约束）、领域知识（可检索上下文）和技能（工具使用程序）外部化为持久、互锁的工件；2) 角色分离的多智能体设计，其中确定性验证器和审计交接在不可逆步骤前的信任边界恢复故障停止语义。

Result: 在两个生产部署中验证：大学GIS中心生态档案（849个数据集）作为单智能体基线；SF2Bench复合洪水基准（2,452个监测站和8,557个文件）验证多智能体工作流。多智能体方法提高了效率（单操作员2天完成，工件跨部署重复使用）和可靠性：审计交接检测并阻止了影响所有2,452个站的坐标转换错误。代表性事件（ISS-004）展示了边界遏制，检测延迟10分钟，零用户暴露，80分钟解决。

Conclusion: EnviSmart通过架构方法将可靠性作为系统属性，成功将LLM驱动的智能体集成到生产环境数据管理中，在概率性工作流中实现确定性可靠性，防止错误传播到不可逆操作，论文已被PEARC 2026接受。

Abstract: Embedding LLM-driven agents into environmental FAIR data management is compelling - they can externalize operational knowledge and scale curation across heterogeneous data and evolving conventions. However, replacing deterministic components with probabilistic workflows changes the failure mode: LLM pipelines may generate plausible but incorrect outputs that pass superficial checks and propagate into irreversible actions such as DOI minting and public release. We introduce EnviSmart, a production data management system deployed on campus-wide storage infrastructure for environmental research. EnviSmart treats reliability as an architectural property through two mechanisms: a three-track knowledge architecture that externalizes behaviors (governance constraints), domain knowledge (retrievable context), and skills (tool-using procedures) as persistent, interlocking artifacts; and a role-separated multi-agent design where deterministic validators and audited handoffs restore fail-stop semantics at trust boundaries before irreversible steps. We compare two production deployments. The University's GIS Center Ecological Archive (849 curated datasets) serves as a single-agent baseline. SF2Bench, a compound flooding benchmark comprising 2,452 monitoring stations and 8,557 published files spanning 39 years, validates the multi-agent workflow. The multi-agent approach improved both efficiency - completed by a single operator in two days with repeated artifact reuse across deployments - and reliability: audited handoffs detected and blocked a coordinate transformation error affecting all 2,452 stations before publication. A representative incident (ISS-004) demonstrated boundary-based containment with 10-minute detection latency, zero user exposure, and 80-minute resolution. This paper has been accepted at PEARC 2026.

</details>


### [157] [CORAL: Towards Autonomous Multi-Agent Evolution for Open-Ended Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01658)
*Ao Qu,Han Zheng,Zijian Zhou,Yihao Yan,Yihong Tang,Shao Yong Ong,Fenglu Hong,Kaichen Zhou,Chonghe Jiang,Minwei Kong,Jiacheng Zhu,Xuan Jiang,Sirui Li,Cathy Wu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Jinhua Zhao,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CORAL：首个用于开放式问题的自主多智能体进化框架，通过共享持久内存、异步执行和心跳干预取代固定启发式规则，在数学、算法和系统优化任务上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的进化方法仍严重依赖固定启发式和硬编码探索规则，限制了智能体的自主性，需要更自主的框架来支持开放式发现中的持续搜索和知识积累。

Method: CORAL框架采用长时运行智能体，通过共享持久内存、异步多智能体执行和心跳干预实现探索、反思和协作，并提供隔离工作空间、评估器分离、资源管理等安全措施。

Result: 在多样化数学、算法和系统优化任务上，CORAL在10个任务上取得新的SOTA结果，改进率比固定进化搜索基线高3-10倍且评估次数更少；在Anthropic内核工程任务中，四个协同进化智能体将最佳得分从1363周期提升至1103周期。

Conclusion: 增强的智能体自主性和多智能体进化能显著改善开放式发现，CORAL通过知识重用和多智能体探索通信机制实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based evolution is a promising approach for open-ended discovery, where progress requires sustained search and knowledge accumulation. Existing methods still rely heavily on fixed heuristics and hard-coded exploration rules, which limit the autonomy of LLM agents. We present CORAL, the first framework for autonomous multi-agent evolution on open-ended problems. CORAL replaces rigid control with long-running agents that explore, reflect, and collaborate through shared persistent memory, asynchronous multi-agent execution, and heartbeat-based interventions. It also provides practical safeguards, including isolated workspaces, evaluator separation, resource management, and agent session and health management.
